This is especially a common problem for the multicarrier systems, e.g., OFDM used in 4G, 5G, and possibly also in 6G, which is characterized by a high Peak-to-Average Power Ratio. While the impact of a nonlinear Power Amplifier (PA) on an OFDM signal is well characterized, it is a relatively new topic for the M-MIMO OFDM systems. Most of the recent works either neglect nonlinear effects or utilize simplified models proper for Rayleigh or LoS radio channel models. In this paper, we first theoretically characterize the nonlinear distortion in the M-MIMO system under commonly used radio channel models. Then, utilizing 3D-Ray Tracing (3D-RT) software, we demonstrate that these models are not very accurate. Instead, we propose two models: a statistical one and an ML-based one using 3D-RT results. The proposed statistical model utilizes the Generalized Extreme Value (GEV) distribution to model Signal to Distortion Ratio (SDR) for victim users, receiving nonlinear distortion, e.g., as interference from neighboring cells. The proposed ML model aims to predict SDR for a scheduled user (receiving nonlinear distortion along with the desired signal), based on the spatial characteristics of the radio channel and the operation point of each PA feeding at the M-MIMO antenna array. The predicted SDR can then be used to perform PA-aware per-user power allocation. The results show about 12% median gain in user throughput achieved by the proposed ML-based power allocation scheme over the state-of-the-art, fixed operating point scheme.

</details>


### [77] [Corner Reflector Array Jamming Discrimination Using Multi-Dimensional Micro-Motion Features with Frequency Agile Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16008)
*Jie Yuan,Lei Wang,Yanhao Wang,Yimin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微动特征融合的稳健方法，用于频捷变雷达中真实舰船与角反射体阵列干扰的鉴别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在频捷变雷达系统中，真实舰船目标与角反射体阵列干扰难以区分，亟需一种稳健的鉴别方法以提升雷达抗干扰能力。

Method: 从距离-速度图提取两类手工特征（均值加权残差MWR和互补对比因子CCF），将其与轻量级CNN学习的深度特征进行融合，最后通过XGBoost分类器完成真伪目标判别。该方法利用刚性舰船与非刚性诱饵在多维微动特征上的差异进行区分。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，所提出的混合特征集方法在性能上持续优于现有先进替代方案，验证了该方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了真实舰船与角反射体阵列干扰的鉴别问题，具有较强的稳健性和实用价值，为频捷变雷达抗干扰技术提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper introduces a robust discrimination method for distinguishing real ship targets from corner-reflector-array jamming with frequency-agile radar. The key idea is to exploit the multidimensional micro-motion signatures that separate rigid ships from non-rigid decoys. From Range-Velocity maps we derive two new hand-crafted descriptors-mean weighted residual (MWR) and complementary contrast factor (CCF) and fuse them with deep features learned by a lightweight CNN. An XGBoost classifier then gives the final decision. Extensive simulations show that the hybrid feature set consistently outperforms state-of-the-art alternatives, confirming the superiority of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [78] [JumpLoRA: Sparse Adapters for Continual Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16171)
*Alexandra Dragomir,Ioana Pintilie,Antonio Barbalau,Marius Dragoi,Florin Brad,Cristian Daniel Paduraru,Alexandru Tifrea,Elena Burceanu,Radu Tudor Ionescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了JumpLoRA框架，通过JumpReLU门控机制在LoRA模块中自适应引入稀疏性，实现动态参数隔离以防止任务干扰，在持续学习任务中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Adapter的大语言模型持续学习方法通过约束新旧适配器来缓解灾难性遗忘，但性能仍有提升空间。作者旨在通过动态参数隔离机制来更有效地防止任务干扰，进一步提升持续学习性能。

Method: 提出JumpLoRA框架，利用JumpReLU门控机制在低秩适应(LoRA)模块中自适应地诱导稀疏性，实现动态参数隔离。该方法具有高度模块化特性，可与现有的基于LoRA的持续学习方法兼容。

Result: JumpLoRA显著提升了IncLoRA方法的性能，并且优于当前最先进的持续学习方法ELLA，在持续学习任务中取得了更优的表现。

Conclusion: JumpLoRA通过动态参数隔离机制有效解决了持续学习中的任务干扰问题，为基于LoRA的大语言模型持续学习提供了一个高效、模块化的解决方案。

Abstract: Adapter-based methods have become a cost-effective approach to continual learning (CL) for Large Language Models (LLMs), by sequentially learning a low-rank update matrix for each task. To mitigate catastrophic forgetting, state-of-the-art approaches impose constraints on new adapters with respect to the previous ones, by targeting either subspace or coordinate-wise interference. In this paper, we propose JumpLoRA, a novel framework to adaptively induce sparsity in the Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) blocks through the use of JumpReLU gating. The method achieves dynamic parameter isolation, which helps prevent task interference. We demonstrate that our method is highly modular and compatible with LoRA-based CL approaches. Specifically, it significantly boosts the performance of IncLoRA and outperforms the leading state-of-the-art CL method, ELLA.

</details>


### [79] [AEGIS: Anchor-Enforced Gradient Isolation for Knowledge-Preserving Vision-Language-Action Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16067)
*Guransh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AEGIS框架，通过层级正交梯度投影解决视觉-语言模型迁移至机器人控制时的跨模态梯度不对称问题，在实现连续MSE学习的同时保持预训练VQA能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉-语言模型(VLM)在适应机器人控制任务时，需要将流匹配动作专家的高幅连续梯度注入仅经过交叉熵训练的骨干网络。这种低秩MSE回归梯度与CE预训练塑造的高维语义流形之间的光谱维度不匹配，导致VLM的视觉问答(VQA)能力快速、严重退化。现有防御方法（完全切断梯度路径或使用低秩适配器）要么丢弃丰富的连续监督信号，要么仍会覆盖预训练流形。

Method: 提出AEGIS：一种无缓冲区、层级正交梯度投影框架。首先从掩码VQA前向传播中预计算静态高斯参考锚点；训练时构建Wasserstein-2传输惩罚生成锚点恢复梯度；采用顺序双反向传播分解任务梯度和锚点梯度；对每个Transformer层应用Gram-Schmidt正交投影，将任务梯度偏离破坏性方向同时保留其建设性内容。

Result: 该方法平均仅损失不到1%的梯度能量，却消除了导致严重遗忘的累积激活漂移，无需任何协同训练数据或重放缓冲区即可在保持预训练VQA流形的同时实现直接连续MSE学习。

Conclusion: AEGIS成功解决了跨模态梯度不对称问题，为预训练视觉-语言模型迁移至机器人控制任务提供了一种高效且保护原有能力的方法，实现了任务性能与知识保持的有效平衡。

Abstract: Adapting pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) for robotic control requires injecting high-magnitude continuous gradients from a flow-matching action expert into a backbone trained exclusively with cross-entropy. This cross-modal gradient asymmetry - the spectral dimensionality mismatch between low-rank MSE regression gradients and the high-dimensional semantic manifold sculpted by CE pre-training, causes rapid, severe erosion of the VLM's visual-question-answering (VQA) capability. Industry-standard defences either sever the gradient pathway entirely via stop gradient, discarding the rich continuous supervision, or restrict parameter capacity through low-rank adapters (LoRA) that constrain the rank of updates but not their direction, and thus still overwrite the pre-trained manifold. We introduce AEGIS (Anchor-Enforced Gradient Isolation System): a buffer-free, layer-wise orthogonal gradient projection framework that enables direct continuous MSE learning while preserving the pre-trained VQA manifold - without any co-training data or replay buffer. AEGIS pre-computes a static Gaussian reference anchor from masked VQA forward passes across all transformer layers, then at each training step constructs a Wasserstein-2 transport penalty that generates an anchor restoration gradient. A sequential dual-backward decomposes the task and anchor gradients; for each transformer layer, AEGIS applies a single Gram-Schmidt orthogonal projection that bends the task gradient away from the destructive direction while preserving its constructive content. The projection sheds less than 1% of gradient energy on average, yet eliminates the cumulative activation drift that drives severe forgetting.

</details>


### [80] [Synthetic data in cryptocurrencies using generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16182)
*André Saimon S. Sousa,Otto Pires,Frank Acasiete,Oscar M. Granados,Valéria Loureiro da Silva,Hugo Saba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件生成对抗网络（CGAN）的深度学习方法，用于生成加密货币价格时间序列的合成数据，以解决金融数据使用中的隐私风险和访问限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字金融生态系统中，真实数据的使用面临隐私风险和访问限制，影响机构、研究和建模过程。为了解决这些问题，需要一种能够生成统计一致的合成数据的替代方案。

Method: 采用条件生成对抗网络（CGANs）架构，结合LSTM类型的循环生成器和MLP判别器，生成统计一致的合成数据，应用于加密货币价格时间序列。

Result: 实验考虑了多种加密资产，结果表明该模型能够再现相关的时间模式，保留市场趋势和动态。与传统复杂的生成方法相比，该方法的计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 使用GANs生成合成时间序列是模拟金融数据的高效替代方案，在市场行为分析和异常检测等应用中展现出潜力。

Abstract: Data plays a fundamental role in consolidating markets, services, and products in the digital financial ecosystem. However, the use of real data, especially in the financial context, can lead to privacy risks and access restrictions, affecting institutions, research, and modeling processes. Although not all financial datasets present such limitations, this work proposes the use of deep learning techniques for generating synthetic data applied to cryptocurrency price time series. The approach is based on Conditional Generative Adversarial Networks (CGANs), combining an LSTM-type recurrent generator and an MLP discriminator to produce statistically consistent synthetic data. The experiments consider different crypto-assets and demonstrate that the model is capable of reproducing relevant temporal patterns, preserving market trends and dynamics. The generation of synthetic series through GANs is an efficient alternative for simulating financial data, showing potential for applications such as market behavior analysis and anomaly detection, with lower computational cost compared to more complex generative approaches.

</details>


### [81] [Neuro-Symbolic ODE Discovery with Latent Grammar Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16232)
*Karin Yu,Eleni Chatzi,Georgios Kissas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了潜在语法流（LGF），一个神经符号生成框架，用于从数据中发现常微分方程，结合了符号表示的可解释性和生成模型的高效搜索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解和分析自然或工程系统通常依赖微分方程等符号公式，因为它们相比黑盒模型具有更好的可解释性和可迁移性，因此需要一种能从数据中自动发现此类方程的方法。

Method: LGF将方程作为基于语法的表示嵌入到离散潜在空间中，利用行为损失使语义相似的方程在空间中更接近；随后使用离散流模型引导采样过程，递归生成最拟合观测数据的候选方程，并可将稳定性等先验知识嵌入规则或作为条件预测器。

Result: 该框架能够有效地在潜在空间中组织方程结构，并通过流模型引导生成符合观测数据的微分方程，同时支持将领域知识（如稳定性约束）整合到生成过程中。

Conclusion: LGF提供了一种将符号推理与深度生成模型相结合的有效途径，实现了从数据中发现可解释、可迁移且符合物理约束的微分方程模型。

Abstract: Understanding natural and engineered systems often relies on symbolic formulations, such as differential equations, which provide interpretability and transferability beyond black-box models.
  We introduce Latent Grammar Flow (LGF), a neuro-symbolic generative framework for discovering ordinary differential equations from data. LGF embeds equations as grammar-based representations into a discrete latent space and forces semantically similar equations to be positioned closer together with a behavioural loss. Then, a discrete flow model guides the sampling process to recursively generate candidate equations that best fit the observed data. Domain knowledge and constraints, such as stability, can be either embedded into the rules or used as conditional predictors.

</details>


### [82] [Joint-Centric Dual Contrastive Alignment with Structure-Preserving and Information-Balanced Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16247)
*Habibeh Naderi,Behrouz Haji Soleimani,Stan Matwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HILBERT框架，通过分层聚合和互惠双重对比学习，在低资源环境下实现了文档级音频-文本长序列的高效表征学习，有效解决了模态维度不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低资源数据环境下长序列音频-文档的表征学习难题，特别是严重的音频-文本维度不平衡问题，现有方法难以在保持模态特定结构的同时有效对齐模态。

Method: 1) 利用冻结预训练编码器提取片段级特征，通过跨模态注意力和自注意力池化分层聚合形成模态特定和联合文档表示；2) 提出互惠双重对比目标，同时优化音频-联合、文本-联合表示的对齐；3) 引入CKA损失保持结构一致性，互信息平衡损失防止单模态主导；4) 采用混合专家分类器处理异构标签分布。

Result: 在多种音频-文本骨干组合上验证了方法的有效性，能够学习语义丰富的长序列表示，并在高度不平衡多类设置下取得优越性能。

Conclusion: HILBERT通过创新的对比目标和正则化策略，成功实现了低资源环境下长序列音频-文本的有效融合，为文档级多模态表征学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose HILBERT (HIerarchical Long-sequence Balanced Embedding with Reciprocal contrastive Training), a cross-attentive multimodal framework for learning document-level audio-text representations from long, segmented sequences in low-resource data settings. HILBERT leverages frozen pre-trained speech and language encoders to extract segment-level features, which are aggregated via cross-modal attention and self-attentive pooling to form modality-specific document representations and a joint cross-attentive embedding. To align modalities while preserving modality-specific structure under severe audio-text dimensional imbalance, we introduce a reciprocal dual contrastive objective that simultaneously aligns audio-to-joint and text-to-joint representations, rather than directly contrasting audio and text alone. Two auxiliary regularizers further stabilize long-sequence fusion: a Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) loss that preserves structural consistency between each modality and the joint embedding, and a mutual information balancing loss that prevents dominance of a single modality by equalizing information flow from audio and text into the joint space. For downstream prediction, HILBERT employs a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) classifier over concatenated audio, text, and joint representations to accommodate heterogeneous label regimes. Extensive evaluation across multiple audio-text backbone combinations demonstrates that HILBERT learns semantically meaningful long-sequence representations and achieves superior performance on highly imbalanced multi-class settings.

</details>


### [83] [The Harder Path: Last Iterate Convergence for Uncoupled Learning in Zero-Sum Games with Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16087)
*Côme Fiegel,Pierre Ménard,Tadashi Kozuno,Michal Valko,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在零和矩阵博弈中，玩家在不通信的情况下通过Bandit反馈进行重复博弈的问题。研究证明了在非耦合算法下，策略序列收敛到纳什均衡的最佳速率为Ω(T^{-1/4})，并提出了两种达到该最优速率的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零和矩阵博弈中，如何在玩家之间不进行通信（非耦合）且仅有Bandit反馈的情况下，保证策略序列（last-iterate）收敛到纳什均衡是一个重要的研究问题。虽然非Bandit设置已有广泛研究，但Bandit反馈下的研究相对较少，现有结果的收敛速率为O(T^{-1/8})。本文旨在探索该设置下的最优收敛速率及其算法实现。

Method: 本文首先从理论上证明了对于非耦合算法，策略序列收敛到纳什均衡的最佳速率为Ω(T^{-1/4})，明显慢于平均迭代收敛的Ω(T^{-1/2})速率。随后，提出了两种算法来实现这一最优速率：第一种算法利用探索与利用之间的直接权衡；第二种算法采用基于两步镜像下降的正则化技术。

Result: 研究表明，在非耦合算法下，策略序列收敛到纳什均衡的最优速率为Ω(T^{-1/4})，优于现有的O(T^{-1/8})结果。提出的两种算法均能达到这一最优速率（忽略常数和对数因子），填补了该领域的理论空白。

Conclusion: 本文为零和矩阵博弈中Bandit反馈下的非耦合算法提供了理论下界，并设计了两种实用算法以达到最优收敛速率。这些成果不仅深化了对博弈学习理论的理解，也为实际应用中的算法设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning in zero-sum matrix games with repeated play and bandit feedback. Specifically, we focus on developing uncoupled algorithms that guarantee, without communication between players, the convergence of the last-iterate to a Nash equilibrium. Although the non-bandit case has been studied extensively, this setting has only been explored recently, with a bound of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/8})$ on the exploitability gap. We show that, for uncoupled algorithms, guaranteeing convergence of the policy profiles to a Nash equilibrium is detrimental to the performance, with the best attainable rate being $Ω(T^{-1/4})$ in contrast to the usual $Ω(T^{-1/2})$ rate for convergence of the average iterates. We then propose two algorithms that achieve this optimal rate up to constant and logarithmic factors. The first algorithm leverages a straightforward trade-off between exploration and exploitation, while the second employs a regularization technique based on a two-step mirror descent approach.

</details>


### [84] [Beyond Distribution Sharpening: The Importance of Task Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16259)
*Sarthak Mittal,Leo Gagnon,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了分布锐化存在固有局限性和不稳定性，而基于任务奖励的强化学习能带来显著的性能提升和稳定学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对强化学习是否真能赋予基础模型新技能，还是仅仅锐化其现有分布以激发潜在能力的争论，本文旨在通过显式对比分布锐化与任务奖励学习这两种范式来解答这一问题。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，理论分析分布锐化的局限性及其不稳定性的根本原因；使用Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507三个模型在数学数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明分布锐化的最优解可能不利且方法本质上不稳定；实验证实分布锐化带来的提升有限，而引入基于任务的奖励信号能显著提高性能并实现稳定学习。

Conclusion: 强化学习的价值在于利用任务奖励信号进行有效学习，而非简单的分布锐化；基于任务奖励的方法是实现模型能力稳健提升的正确途径。

Abstract: Frontier models have demonstrated exceptional capabilities following the integration of task-reward-based reinforcement learning (RL) into their training pipelines, enabling systems to evolve from pure reasoning models into sophisticated agents. However, debate persists regarding whether RL genuinely instills new skills within a base model or merely sharpens its existing distribution to elicit latent capabilities. To address this dichotomy, we present an explicit comparison between distribution sharpening and task-reward-based learning, utilizing RL as a tool to implement both paradigms. Our analysis reveals the inherent limitations of distribution sharpening, demonstrating from first principles how and why the optima can be unfavorable and the approach fundamentally unstable. Furthermore, our experiments using Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 on math datasets confirm that sharpening yields limited gains, whereas incorporating task-based reward signal can greatly help achieve robust performance improvements and stable learning.

</details>


### [85] [Sample Complexity Bounds for Stochastic Shortest Path with a Generative Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16111)
*Jean Tarbouriech,Matteo Pirotta,Michal Valko,Alessandro Lazaric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机最短路径（SSP）问题的样本复杂度，证明了其学习难度高于有限视界和折扣设置，并提出了匹配下界的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解SSP问题的学习难度，特别是要确定学习一个ε最优策略所需的样本数量下界，以及最小代价为0时的可学习性问题。

Method: 首先推导具有生成模型时的样本复杂度下界，通过构造最坏情况实例证明下界；然后设计两个算法分别匹配一般情况和c_min=0特殊情况的下界（对数因子范围内）。

Result: 证明了任何算法在最坏情况下需要至少Ω(SAB_*³/(c_minε²))个样本才能返回ε最优策略；发现当c_min=0时SSP问题可能不可学习；提出了匹配下界的算法。

Conclusion: SSP问题的学习比有限视界和折扣设置更难，因为c_min=0时可能出现不可学习的情况；但在最优策略到目标状态的有界命中时间条件下，即使c_min=0也可学习。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of learning an $ε$-optimal policy in the Stochastic Shortest Path (SSP) problem. We first derive sample complexity bounds when the learner has access to a generative model. We show that there exists a worst-case SSP instance with $S$ states, $A$ actions, minimum cost $c_{\min}$, and maximum expected cost of the optimal policy over all states $B_{\star}$, where any algorithm requires at least $Ω(SAB_{\star}^3/(c_{\min}ε^2))$ samples to return an $ε$-optimal policy with high probability. Surprisingly, this implies that whenever $c_{\min} = 0$ an SSP problem may not be learnable, thus revealing that learning in SSPs is strictly harder than in the finite-horizon and discounted settings. We complement this lower bound with an algorithm that matches it, up to logarithmic factors, in the general case, and an algorithm that matches it up to logarithmic factors even when $c_{\min} = 0$, but only under the condition that the optimal policy has a bounded hitting time to the goal state.

</details>


### [86] [Univariate Channel Fusion for Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16119)
*Fernando Moro,Vinicius M. A. Souza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UCF方法，通过简单的通道融合策略（如均值、中位数等）将多变量时间序列转换为单变量表示，从而可使用任意单变量分类器。该方法在五个案例研究中表现出色，在保证分类性能的同时显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多变量时间序列分类方法（尤其是深度学习模型）通常需要高昂的计算资源，这限制了其在实时应用或低成本硬件（如IoT设备和可穿戴系统）上的部署。

Method: 提出Univariate Channel Fusion (UCF)方法，通过简单的通道融合策略（如均值、中位数或动态时间规整重心）将多变量时间序列转换为单变量表示。这种转换使得原本为单变量时间序列设计的分类器能够应用于多变量场景，提供了一种灵活且计算轻量级的替代方案。

Result: 在涵盖化学监测、脑机接口和人类活动分析等多个应用领域的五个案例研究中进行了评估。结果表明，UCF通常优于基线方法和专为MTSC定制的最先进算法，同时在计算效率方面取得了显著提升，在具有高通道间相关性的问题上表现尤为有效。

Conclusion: UCF方法为多变量时间序列分类提供了一种高效、轻量级的解决方案，特别适合计算资源受限的场景，能够有效平衡分类性能与计算效率，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Multivariate time series classification (MTSC) plays a crucial role in various domains, including biomedical signal analysis and motion monitoring. However, existing approaches, particularly deep learning models, often require high computational resources, making them unsuitable for real-time applications or deployment on low-cost hardware, such as IoT devices and wearable systems. In this paper, we propose the Univariate Channel Fusion (UCF) method to deal with MTSC efficiently. UCF transforms multivariate time series into a univariate representation through simple channel fusion strategies such as the mean, median, or dynamic time warping barycenter. This transformation enables the use of any classifier originally designed for univariate time series, providing a flexible and computationally lightweight alternative to complex models. We evaluate UCF in five case studies covering diverse application domains, including chemical monitoring, brain-computer interfaces, and human activity analysis. The results demonstrate that UCF often outperforms baseline methods and state-of-the-art algorithms tailored for MTSC, while achieving substantial gains in computational efficiency, being particularly effective in problems with high inter-channel correlation.

</details>


### [87] [Tabular foundation models for in-context prediction of molecular properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16123)
*Karim K. Ben Hicham,Jan G. Rittig,Martin Grohe,Alexander Mitsos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了表格基础模型（TFMs）在小到中等规模数据下的分子性质预测能力。TFMs通过上下文学习进行推理，无需特定任务微调。结合CheMeleon嵌入，TFMs在30个MoleculeACE任务上实现了高达100%的胜率，相比传统微调方法具有更高的预测精度和更低的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测对药物发现、催化和工艺设计至关重要，但实际应用常受限于小数据集。现有分子基础模型通常需要特定任务微调和机器学习专业知识，且往往难以超越经典基线方法。为了解决这些限制，研究者探索了表格基础模型（TFMs）这一全新范式，该范式通过上下文学习实现无需任务特定训练的推理。

Method: 研究者在低到中等数据范围内评估了TFMs的性能，涵盖了标准化药物基准测试和化学工程数据集。评估内容包括冻结的分子基础模型表示（如CheMeleon嵌入）、经典描述符（RDKit2d、Mordred）和分子指纹等多种分子表示方法。通过系统比较TFMs与微调方法在预测性能和计算成本上的差异，验证了TFMs在实际应用中的有效性。

Result: TFMs在各项基准测试中表现出优秀的预测性能，同时相比微调方法降低了计算成本。特别是TFMs结合CheMeleon嵌入在30个MoleculeACE任务上实现了高达100%的胜率；紧凑的RDKit2d和Mordred描述符也提供了强有力的基于描述符的替代方案。分子表示被证明是TFM性能的关键决定因素，分子基础模型嵌入和2D描述符集在许多任务上相比经典分子指纹都有显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于上下文学习的TFMs为实际应用中的分子性质预测提供了一种高精度且成本高效的替代方案。分子表示的选择对TFM性能至关重要，分子基础模型嵌入和2D描述符集均优于经典分子指纹。这一发现为小数据环境下的分子性质预测开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Accurate molecular property prediction is central to drug discovery, catalysis, and process design, yet real-world applications are often limited by small datasets. Molecular foundation models provide a promising direction by learning transferable molecular representations; however, they typically involve task-specific fine-tuning, require machine learning expertise, and often fail to outperform classical baselines. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) offer a fundamentally different paradigm: they perform predictions through in-context learning, enabling inference without task-specific training. Here, we evaluate TFMs in the low- to medium-data regime across both standardized pharmaceutical benchmarks and chemical engineering datasets. We evaluate both frozen molecular foundation model representations, as well as classical descriptors and fingerprints. Across the benchmarks, the approach shows excellent predictive performance while reducing computational cost, compared to fine-tuning, with these advantages also transferring to practical engineering data settings. In particular, combining TFMs with CheMeleon embeddings yields up to 100\% win rates on 30 MoleculeACE tasks, while compact RDKit2d and Mordred descriptors provide strong descriptor-based alternatives. Molecular representation emerges as a key determinant in TFM performance, with molecular foundation model embeddings and 2D descriptor sets both providing substantial gains over classic molecular fingerprints on many tasks. These results suggest that in-context learning with TFMs provides a highly accurate and cost-efficient alternative for property prediction in practical applications.

</details>


### [88] [Sketching the Readout of Large Language Models for Scalable Data Attribution and Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16197)
*Yide Ran,Jianwen Xie,Minghui Wang,Wenjin Zheng,Denghui Zhang,Chuan Li,Zhaozhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RISE方法，通过聚焦输出层的影响热点并结合CountSketch投影，实现了LLM的高效数据归因与估值，在保持准确性的同时将索引存储减少112倍，并成功扩展至32B参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于梯度的数据归因方法在大型语言模型（LLM）上存在可扩展性挑战，因为计算和存储整个模型的梯度开销巨大。受人类认知决策过程的启发（仅依赖相关记忆的聚焦读取而非重放所有路径），作者旨在开发一种高效且可扩展的LLM数据归因与估值方法。

Method: 提出了RISE（Readout Influence Sketching Estimator）。该方法不计算整个LLM的梯度，而是聚焦于影响信号集中的输出层，利用梯度的分解外积形式构建双通道表示：词汇残差通道（RH）和语义投影误差通道（GH）。随后应用CountSketch投影对这两个通道进行压缩，从而实现高效存储。

Result: 在OLMo（1B-32B）和Pythia（14M-6.9B）模型家族上的实验表明，RISE相比RapidIn减少了高达112倍的索引存储，并成功扩展至32B参数的LLM，而RapidIn和ZO-Inf等基线方法在该规模下因内存限制不可行。在回溯性归因（检索影响训练样本）和前瞻性估值（零样本评分候选数据）两种范式下均得到验证。在Howdy后门检测、Finance-Medical领域分离和Brain Rot高质量数据选择三项任务中表现优异。闭环研究中，使用RISE选择的数据进行持续预训练带来了持续的下游性能提升。

Conclusion: RISE为现代大语言模型的影响分析和训练数据选择提供了一种实用且可扩展的基础工具，有效解决了现有方法在大模型上的存储和计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Data attribution and valuation are critical for understanding data-model synergy for Large Language Models (LLMs), yet existing gradient-based methods suffer from scalability challenges on LLMs. Inspired by human cognition, where decision making relies on a focused readout of relevant memories rather than replaying all pathways, we introduce RISE (Readout Influence Sketching Estimator). Instead of computing and indexing gradients across the entire LLM, RISE focuses on influence hotspots at the output layer, where influence signals concentrate, and the gradient admits a decomposed outer-product form. This enables a dual-channel representation combining a lexical residual channel (RH) and a semantic projected-error channel (GH). Applying CountSketch projections to these channels achieves strong compression while maintaining accurate attribution. Across the OLMo (1B-32B) and Pythia (14M-6.9B) families, RISE reduces index storage by up to 112$\times$ compared to RapidIn and scales to 32B parameters LLM, where gradient-based baselines such as RapidIn and ZO-Inf become memory-infeasible. We evaluate RISE on two paradigms: (1) retrospective attribution, retrieving influential training examples for specific predictions, and (2) prospective valuation, scoring candidate data utility zero-shot. We validate RISE on three tasks: Howdy backdoor data detection, Finance-Medical domain separation, and Brain Rot high-quality data selection. In a closed-loop Brain Rot study, continued pretraining on RISE-selected data yields consistent downstream improvements. Overall, RISE provides a practical and scalable primitive for influence analysis and training-data selection in modern large language models.

</details>


### [89] [OT on the Map: Quantifying Domain Shifts in Geographic Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16220)
*Haoran Zhang,Livia Betti,Konstantin Klemmer,Esther Rolf,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoSpOT，一种利用地理信息和最优传输方法计算地理空间域之间距离的策略，该距离能有效预测跨域迁移的难度，并支持模型训练和部署决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地理数据计算机视觉和机器学习中，由于全球数据覆盖不均和区域间的分布偏移，域外泛化是一个普遍挑战。目前缺乏一种原则性的方法来判断模型在一个区域训练后部署到另一个区域是否能成功，因此需要一种定义良好的分布间距离度量来量化目标域与训练域的差异。

Method: 提出了一种名为GeoSpOT（Geographic Spatial Optimal Transport）的策略，结合地理信息与最优传输（Optimal Transport）方法来计算地理空间域之间的距离。该方法利用预训练位置编码器的嵌入表示，仅需经纬度对作为输入，即可获得与图像/文本嵌入相当的信息。

Result: 实验表明：1）GeoSpOT距离是跨域迁移困难度的有效预测指标；2）预训练位置编码器的嵌入提供的信息与图像/文本嵌入相当；3）即使下游任务未知或无任务特定数据，也能估算地理空间模型的域外性能；4）GeoSpOT距离可指导数据选择，并预测模型可能表现不佳的区域。

Conclusion: GeoSpOT距离为地理空间跨域迁移提供了有效的量化工具，能够提前指导数据选择决策，并帮助识别模型可能表现不佳的区域，从而支持地理空间模型的训练和部署优化。

Abstract: In computer vision and machine learning for geographic data, out-of-domain generalization is a pervasive challenge, arising from uneven global data coverage and distribution shifts across geographic regions. Though models are frequently trained in one region and deployed in another, there is no principled method for determining when this cross-region adaptation will be successful. A well-defined notion of distance between distributions can effectively quantify how different a new target domain is compared to the domains used for model training, which in turn could support model training and deployment decisions. In this paper, we propose a strategy for computing distances between geospatial domains that leverages geographic information with Optimal Transport methods (GeoSpOT). In our experiments, GeoSpOT distances emerge as effective predictors of cross-domain transfer difficulty. We further demonstrate that embeddings from pretrained location encoders provide information comparable to image/text embeddings, despite relying solely on longitude-latitude pairs as input. This allows users to get an approximation of out-of-domain performance for geospatial models, even when the exact downstream task is unknown, or no task-specific data is available. Building on these findings, we show that GeoSpOT distances can preemptively guide data selection and enable predictive tools to analyze regions where a model is likely to underperform.

</details>


### [90] [Enhancing AI and Dynamical Subseasonal Forecasts with Probabilistic Bias Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16238)
*Hannah Guan,Soukayna Mouatadid,Paulo Orenstein,Judah Cohen,Haiyu Dong,Zekun Ni,Jeremy Berman,Genevieve Flaspohler,Alex Lu,Jakob Schloer,Joshua Talib,Jonathan A. Weyn,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出概率偏差校正（PBC）机器学习框架，有效解决2-6周次季节性预测精度骤降问题，在ECMWF 2025实时预测竞赛中击败所有对手获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者依赖天气预报进行农作物种植、野火管理、水电资源分配及极端天气准备，虽然现有动力模型和AI模型在两周内预测精度很高，但在次季节性时间尺度（2-6周）模型技能因累积误差和持续偏差而急剧下降，亟需解决方案。

Method: 提出概率偏差校正（PBC）机器学习框架，通过学习校正历史概率预测来大幅减少系统误差，该方法专为业务部署而设计，可应用于动力模型和AI模型。

Result: PBC将AI预测系统的次季节性技能提高一倍，对业务去偏差动力模型在91%气压、92%温度和98%降水目标上提升技能；在ECMWF 2025实时预测竞赛中，其全球预报在所有天气变量和预报时效均排名第一，超越6个业务预报中心的动力模型、国际动力多模式集合、ECMWF的AI预测系统及全球34个团队的预测系统。

Conclusion: 概率技能提升转化为对极端事件更准确的预测，有望改善农业规划、能源管理和脆弱社区的灾害准备工作，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Decision-makers rely on weather forecasts to plant crops, manage wildfires, allocate water and energy, and prepare for weather extremes. Today, such forecasts enjoy unprecedented accuracy out to two weeks thanks to steady advances in physics-based dynamical models and data-driven artificial intelligence (AI) models. However, model skill drops precipitously at subseasonal timescales (2 - 6 weeks ahead), due to compounding errors and persistent biases. To counter this degradation, we introduce probabilistic bias correction (PBC), a machine learning framework that substantially reduces systematic error by learning to correct historical probabilistic forecasts. When applied to the leading dynamical and AI models from the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts (ECMWF), PBC doubles the subseasonal skill of the AI Forecasting System and improves the skill of the operationally-debiased dynamical model for 91% of pressure, 92% of temperature, and 98% of precipitation targets. We designed PBC for operational deployment, and, in ECMWF's 2025 real-time forecasting competition, its global forecasts placed first for all weather variables and lead times, outperforming the dynamical models from six operational forecasting centers, an international dynamical multi-model ensemble, ECMWF's AI Forecasting System, and the forecasting systems of 34 teams worldwide. These probabilistic skill gains translate into more accurate prediction of extreme events and have the potential to improve agricultural planning, energy management, and disaster preparedness in vulnerable communities.

</details>


### [91] [Detecting and Suppressing Reward Hacking with Gradient Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16242)
*Songtao Wang,Quang Hieu Pham,Fangcong Yin,Xinpeng Wang,Jocelyn Qiaochu Chen,Greg Durrett,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRIFT方法，通过分析模型内部梯度表示来检测强化学习中的奖励黑客行为，在多个推理基准测试上相比现有方法提升超过25%，并能提升模型在真实任务目标上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有可验证奖励的强化学习(RLVR)中，模型可能通过利用奖励函数漏洞（奖励黑客）获得高分而未真正解决任务。这种作弊行为往往是隐式的，中间推理链表面看似合理，纯文本监控难以有效识别。

Method: 提出梯度指纹(GRIFT)方法：给定提示和模型生成的思维链，计算基于提示条件的思维链梯度，将其压缩为紧凑表示，利用该梯度级表示评估思维链是否反映奖励黑客行为。该方法利用模型内部计算而非表面文本来进行检测。

Result: 在数学、代码和逻辑推理等可验证推理基准测试中，GRIFT显著优于CoT Monitor和TRACE等强基线方法，在检测奖励黑客行为方面实现超过25%的相对提升。将其集成到拒绝微调流程中可减少奖励黑客行为并提升真实任务目标的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明了利用梯度级表示评估思维链推理轨迹质量的有前景方向，为强化学习中奖励黑客检测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically optimizes for outcome rewards without imposing constraints on intermediate reasoning. This leaves training susceptible to reward hacking, where models exploit loopholes (e.g., spurious patterns in training data) in the reward function to achieve high scores without solving the intended task. These reward-hacking behaviors are often implicit, as the intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) may appear plausible on the surface, limiting the effectiveness of purely text-based monitoring. We propose Gradient Fingerprint (GRIFT), a method for detecting reward hacking using models' internal computations. Given a prompt and a model-generated CoT, GRIFT computes gradients of the CoT conditioned on the prompt and compresses them into a compact representation, which is then used to assess whether the CoT reflects reward hacking behavior. Across verifiable reasoning benchmarks spanning math, code, and logical reasoning, GRIFT substantially outperforms strong baselines, including CoT Monitor and TRACE, achieving over 25% relative improvement in detecting reward hacking behavior. Moreover, integrating GRIFT into the rejection fine-tuning pipeline for reasoning tasks reduces reward hacking and improves performance on the true task objective. Our results highlight a promising direction of leveraging gradient level representations for assessing the quality of CoT reasoning traces. Our code is available at: https://github.com/songtao-x/reward_hack.

</details>


### [92] [FL-MHSM: Spatially-adaptive Fusion and Ensemble Learning for Flood-Landslide Multi-Hazard Susceptibility Mapping at Regional Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16265)
*Aswathi Mundayatt,Jaya Sreevalsan-Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Early Fusion、Late Fusion和Mixture of Experts的深度学习工作流，用于联合洪水-滑坡多灾害易发性制图。实验证明MoE模型在空间异质性区域中表现最佳，有效提升了预测精度并支持跨灾害依赖性和不确定性的表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多灾害易发性制图研究通常依赖空间均匀模型，将各类灾害独立处理，且对跨灾害依赖性和不确定性的表征有限。这些局限性制约了在复杂地形条件下多灾害风险评估的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 提出了一个深度学习工作流用于联合洪水-滑坡多灾害易发性制图（FL-MHSM）。该方法包含两级空间分区、概率Early Fusion（EF）、基于树的Late Fusion（LF）基准模型，以及以软门控Mixture of Experts（MoE）作为最终预测模型。设计通过区域分区保留空间异质性，并使用重叠网格实现数据并行的大区域预测。

Result: 在Kerala地区，EF相比LF将洪水召回率从0.816提升至0.840，Brier分数从0.092降至0.086；MoE在洪水易发性方面表现最强，AUC-ROC达0.905，召回率0.930，F1分数0.722。在Nepal地区，EF将洪水召回率从0.820提升至0.858，Brier分数从0.057降至0.049；MoE在滑坡易发性方面最优，AUC-ROC为0.914，召回率0.901，F1分数0.559。GeoDetector分析显示Kerala的主导因子跨区域变化更大，受地形、土地覆盖和排水相关控制因素的不同组合影响；而Nepal则表现出地形和冰川相关因素更一致的影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明Early Fusion和Late Fusion提供互补的预测行为，通过Mixture of Experts进行空间自适应整合可为FL-MHSM提供稳健的整体预测性能，同时支持对空间异质性景观中多灾害易发性的可解释性表征。该框架有效解决了传统方法在跨灾害依赖性建模和空间异质性处理方面的不足。

Abstract: Existing multi-hazard susceptibility mapping (MHSM) studies often rely on spatially uniform models, treat hazards independently, and provide limited representation of cross-hazard dependence and uncertainty. To address these limitations, this study proposes a deep learning (DL) workflow for joint flood-landslide multi-hazard susceptibility mapping (FL-MHSM) that combines two-level spatial partitioning, probabilistic Early Fusion (EF), a tree-based Late Fusion (LF) baseline, and a soft-gating Mixture of Experts (MoE) model, with MoE serving as final predictive model. The proposed design preserves spatial heterogeneity through zonal partitions and enables data-parallel large-area prediction using overlapping lattice grids. In Kerala, EF remained competitive with LF, improving flood recall from 0.816 to 0.840 and reducing Brier score from 0.092 to 0.086, while MoE provided strongest performance for flood susceptibility, achieving an AUC-ROC of 0.905, recall of 0.930, and F1-score of 0.722. In Nepal, EF similarly improved flood recall from 0.820 to 0.858 and reduced Brier score from 0.057 to 0.049 relative to LF, while MoE outperformed both EF and LF for landslide susceptibility, achieving an AUC-ROC of 0.914, recall of 0.901, and F1-score of 0.559. GeoDetector analysis of MoE outputs further showed that dominant factors varied more across zones in Kerala, where susceptibility was shaped by different combinations of topographic, land-cover, and drainage-related controls, while Nepal showed a more consistent influence of topographic and glacier-related factors across zones. These findings show that EF and LF provide complementary predictive behavior, and that their spatially adaptive integration through MoE yields robust overall predictive performance for FL-MHSM while supporting interpretable characterization of multi-hazard susceptibility in spatially heterogeneous landscapes.

</details>


### [93] [Evaluating the Progression of Large Language Model Capabilities for Small-Molecule Drug Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16279)
*Shriram Chennakesavalu,Kirill Shmilovich,Hayley Weir,Colin Grambow,John Bradshaw,Patricia Suriana,Chen Cheng,Kangway Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套基于强化学习的化学任务基准，发现前沿大模型在化学任务上表现日益提升但仍有改进空间，通过强化学习后训练可显著提升较小模型的性能，使其媲美最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在药物设计领域具有潜力，但缺乏反映真实场景的基准测试，其实际效用尚不明确，因此需要建立真实有效的评估体系。

Method: 构建了一套化学基础任务（包括分子性质预测、分子表示转换和分子设计），并将其形式化为强化学习（RL）环境，实现统一评估与后训练。

Result: 前沿模型在化学任务上表现日益提升，但在低数据实验场景下仍有显著改进空间；通过RL后训练，较小的模型能显著提升性能，达到与最先进前沿模型相媲美的水平。

Conclusion: 结合精心设计的评估任务与针对性后训练，可有效揭示并缩小LLMs在药物发现中的能力差距，为LLMs在药物发现中的实际应用提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have the potential to accelerate small molecule drug design due to their ability to reason about information from diverse sources and formats. However, their practical utility remains unclear due to the lack of benchmarks that reflect real-world scenarios. In this work, we introduce a suite of chemically-grounded tasks spanning molecular property prediction, molecular representation transformations, and molecular design. Importantly, we formulate these tasks as reinforcement learning (RL) environments, enabling a unified approach for evaluation and post-training. Across three model families, we find that frontier models are increasingly proficient at chemical tasks, but that there is significant room for improvement, especially in experimental settings with low data. Critically, we show that RL-based post-training can substantially improve performance. A smaller model post-trained on our environments becomes competitive with state-of-the-art frontier models, despite a significantly weaker base model. This suggests a practical route toward employing LLMs in drug discovery; by combining carefully-designed evaluation tasks with targeted post-training, we can both elucidate and close critical capability gaps.

</details>


### [94] [Geometric regularization of autoencoders via observed stochastic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16282)
*Sean Hill,Felix X. -F. Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切丛约束的三阶段学习方法，用于从短时高维数据中构建随机动力系统的低维约化模拟器，显著降低了首达时间和系数误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有慢速或亚稳态行为的随机动力系统在长时间尺度上演化在未知的低维流形上。现有的局部图表方法（如ATLAS）存在指数级地标扩展和每步重投影问题，而自编码器方法对切丛几何约束不足，导致学习到的漂移和扩散项存在误差传播。

Method: 利用环境协方差Λ编码的坐标不变切空间信息，构建切丛惩罚项和逆一致性惩罚项，设计三阶段流程：图表学习、潜在漂移学习、潜在扩散学习。该方法学习单个非线性图表和潜在随机微分方程，诱导出ρ-度量函数空间度量。针对漂移项，通过Itô公式推导编码器拉回目标，并证明偏置分解。

Result: 在嵌入至201维环境的四个表面上进行实验，旋转动力学下径向平均首达时间误差降低50-70%，在亚稳态Müller-Brown Langevin动力学的大多数表面-转移对上实现最低的井间MFPT误差，相比未正则化自编码器，端到端环境系数误差降低达一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法在W^{2,∞}图表收敛假设下，图表级误差可控地传播到环境动力学的弱收敛和径向平均首达时间的收敛，为高维随机动力系统的降维模拟提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Stochastic dynamical systems with slow or metastable behavior evolve, on long time scales, on an unknown low-dimensional manifold in high-dimensional ambient space. Building a reduced simulator from short-burst ambient ensembles is a long-standing problem: local-chart methods like ATLAS suffer from exponential landmark scaling and per-step reprojection, while autoencoder alternatives leave tangent-bundle geometry poorly constrained, and the errors propagate into the learned drift and diffusion. We observe that the ambient covariance~$Λ$ already encodes coordinate-invariant tangent-space information, its range spanning the tangent bundle. Using this, we construct a tangent-bundle penalty and an inverse-consistency penalty for a three-stage pipeline (chart learning, latent drift, latent diffusion) that learns a single nonlinear chart and the latent SDE. The penalties induce a function-space metric, the $ρ$-metric, strictly weaker than the Sobolev $H^1$ norm yet achieving the same chart-quality generalization rate up to logarithmic factors. For the drift, we derive an encoder-pullback target via Itô's formula on the learned encoder and prove a bias decomposition showing the standard decoder-side formula carries systematic error for any imperfect chart. Under a $W^{2,\infty}$ chart-convergence assumption, chart-level error propagates controllably to weak convergence of the ambient dynamics and to convergence of radial mean first-passage times. Experiments on four surfaces embedded in up to $201$ ambient dimensions reduce radial MFPT error by $50$--$70\%$ under rotation dynamics and achieve the lowest inter-well MFPT error on most surface--transition pairs under metastable Müller--Brown Langevin dynamics, while reducing end-to-end ambient coefficient errors by up to an order of magnitude relative to an unregularized autoencoder.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Taming Asynchronous CPU-GPU Coupling for Frequency-aware Latency Estimation on Mobile Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15357)
*Jiesong Chen,Jun You,Zhidan Liu,Zhenjiang Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLAME框架，通过细粒度的层级建模准确估计模型在不同频率组合下的推理延迟，将DNN的画像时间从数小时缩短至数分钟，SLM的画像时间从数天缩短至数分钟，并在保证延迟预测精度的同时提升了功耗效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在移动边缘应用中，精确估计模型推理延迟对于满足时间约束至关重要。然而，动态电压频率调整（DVFS）的普及使得传统的静态画像方法失效，因为推理延迟会随处理器频率变化而波动。针对频率组合进行全面画像理论上可行但成本极高，特别是对于新兴的小型语言模型（SLMs），可变上下文长度使得画像时间长达数天。简单的分析缩放方法因CPU与GPU间复杂的异步耦合关系而无法准确预测延迟波动。

Method: 提出了FLAME框架，采用自底向上的层级建模方法。该方法量化了重叠的并行性，并在扩展到完整模型时聚合由异步处理器交互引起的动态流水线气泡。这种建模方式确保了从深度神经网络到小型语言模型的通用性，其精确的建模允许仅对稀疏的样本子集进行画像，从而大幅减少画像时间。

Result: FLAME将DNN的画像时间从数小时缩短至数分钟，将SLM的画像时间从数天缩短至数分钟，同时在跨频率估计中保持较小的误差。在延迟感知的DVFS应用中，FLAME在功耗效率和延迟保证方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FLAME通过创新的层级建模方法有效解决了DVFS环境下模型推理延迟估计的难题，实现了跨频率组合的高精度延迟预测，大幅降低了画像成本，并在实际应用中展示了其在功耗优化和延迟保证方面的优越性能，为时间敏感的移动边缘应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Precise estimation of model inference latency is crucial for time-critical mobile edge applications, enabling devices to calculate latency margins against deadlines and trade them for enhanced model performance or resource savings. However, the ubiquity of Dynamic Voltage and Frequency Scaling (DVFS) renders traditional static profiling invalid in real-world deployments, as inference latency fluctuates with varying processor (CPU and GPU) frequencies. While extensive profiling across frequency combinations is theoretically possible, it is prohibitively expensive, particularly for emerging Small Language Models (SLMs), where variable context lengths explode the profiling up to days. We observe that simple analytic scaling fails to predict these fluctuations due to the complex asynchronous coupling between CPU (kernel launching) and GPU (execution). In this paper, we introduce FLAME to accurately estimate inference latency across frequency combinations. It features a novel layer-wise modeling that quantifies the overlapping parallelism and then aggregates dynamic pipeline bubbles caused by asynchronous processor interactions when extending to the full model. This bottom-up approach ensures generalizability across diverse models from DNNs to SLMs, and its precise modeling allows for profiling a sparse subset of samples, cutting DNN profiling from hours to minutes and SLM profiling from days to mere minutes, while maintaining small estimation errors across frequencies. We further showcase FLAME's utility in a deadline-aware DVFS, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach in both power efficiency and latency guarantees.

</details>


### [96] [AutoFlows++: Hierarchical Message Flow Mining for System on Chip Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15359)
*Bardia Nadimi,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AutoFlows++框架，通过“局部挖掘+全局挖掘”的两阶段层级方法，有效解决了复杂SoC通信轨迹中因交错实例和因果关系模糊导致的消息流提取难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代SoC设计中，从通信轨迹中提取简洁的通信规约对于功能验证、性能分析和后硅调试至关重要。然而，由于通信流实例的交错以及消息间因果关系的模糊性，现有挖掘方法在面对多组件间复杂的消息模式交错时，常面临可扩展性差和歧义性问题，导致候选流数量爆炸及提取行为不准确。

Method: 提出了一个受设计架构指导的层级框架AutoFlows++，分为两个阶段：1. 局部挖掘阶段，从组件间单个通信接口观察到的轨迹中提取简单通信模式；2. 全局挖掘阶段，将局部模式进行组合，以识别描述跨多组件通信行为的高层消息流。

Result: 在合成轨迹及GEM5中SoC模型生成的轨迹上的实验结果表明，AutoFlows++相较于现有方法显著提升了流提取的准确性，证明了其在实际SoC验证任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: AutoFlows++通过分层级的挖掘策略成功应对了复杂SoC通信轨迹分析的挑战，显著提升了消息流提取的准确性，为SoC验证提供了有效的工具。

Abstract: Understanding communication behavior in modern system-on-chip (SoC) designs is critical for functional verification, performance analysis, and post-silicon debugging. Communication traces capture message exchanges among system components and provide valuable insights into system behavior. However, deriving concise communication specifications from such traces remains challenging due to interleaved instances of communication flows, and ambiguous causal relationships among messages. Existing mining approaches often struggle with scalability and ambiguity when traces contain complex interleaving of message patterns across multiple components. These conditions often lead to an explosion in the number of candidate flows and inaccurate extraction of communication behaviors. This paper presents AutoFlows++, a design-architecture-guided hierarchical framework for mining message flows from communication traces of complex SoC designs. AutoFlows++ operates in two stages: local mining followed by global mining. In the local mining stage, simple communication patterns are extracted from traces observed at individual communication interfaces between components. In the global mining stage, these local patterns are composed to identify higher-level message flows that characterize communication behavior across multiple components. Experimental results on both synthetic traces and traces generated from SoC models in GEM5 demonstrate that AutoFlows++ significantly improves flow extraction accuracy compared with prior approaches, highlighting its effectiveness for practical SoC validation tasks.

</details>


### [97] [GEN-Graph: Heterogeneous PIM Accelerator for General Computational Patterns in Graph-based Dynamic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15361)
*Yanru Chen,Runyang Tian,Zheyu Li,Mahbod Afarin,Weihong Xu,Tajana Rosing*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GEN-Graph，一种异构PIM芯片架构，通过集成Matrix-tile和Traversal-tile两种专用计算单元，统一加速矩阵中心型和拓扑中心型两类图动态规划算法，实现了显著的性能与能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图动态规划是基因组学和网络分析的基石，但存在两类计算模式冲突：矩阵中心型DP（如APSP）具有规律性、计算密集的特点，而拓扑中心型DP（如DNA序列比对）具有不规则、内存密集的特点。这种差异使得单一同构的存内计算（PIM）架构难以高效同时支持两者。

Method: 设计了一种名为GEN-Graph的异构PIM芯片，在2.5D封装内集成两类专用计算单元：Matrix-tile采用存内处理（PUM）架构优化矩阵中心型负载，Traversal-tile采用近存处理（PNM）架构优化遍历中心型负载。软硬件协同设计采用递归划分和可重构窗口化位并行逻辑以确保精确计算。

Result: Matrix-tile在APSP任务上相比NVIDIA H100 GPU实现了42.8倍加速和392倍能效提升；Traversal-tile在序列到图比对任务上，短读长吞吐量达256万reads/s，长读长达3.93万reads/s，相比最先进加速器吞吐量提升高达2.56倍。

Conclusion: GEN-Graph通过将硬件专用化与算法结构相匹配，提供了首个可扩展、精确的通用DP数据流解决方案，有效解决了图动态规划中计算模式冲突的问题。

Abstract: While graph-based dynamic programming (DP) is a cornerstone of genomics and network analytics, its efficiency is hampered by fundamentally conflicting computational patterns. Matrix-centric DP drives regular, compute-bound network analytics, while topology-centric DP handles irregular, memory-bound genomic traversals. These two categories of DP have substantially different computation patterns and dataflows, which makes it difficult for a single homogeneous processing-in-memory (PIM) architecture to efficiently support both.
  This work presents GEN-Graph, a novel heterogeneous PIM chiplet that integrates two types of specialized compute tiles within a 2.5D package: Matrix-tile, a processing-using-memory (PUM) tile optimized for matrix-centric workloads, such as all-pairs shortest path (APSP); and traversal-tile, a processing-near-memory (PNM) tile optimized for traversal-centric DP workloads, such as DNA sequence alignment. Our hardware-software co-design employs recursive partitioning and reconfigurable windowed bit-parallel logic to ensure exact computation. Results show the matrix tile achieves 42.8x speedup and 392x energy efficiency over the NVIDIA H100 GPU for APSP. For sequence-to-graph alignment, the traversal tile sustains 2.56 million reads/s (short-reads) and 39.3 thousand reads/s (long-reads), outperforming state-of-the-art accelerators by up to 2.56x in throughput. GEN-Graph provides the first scalable, exact solution for general DP dataflows by matching hardware specialization to algorithmic structure.

</details>


### [98] [Photonic AI: A Hybrid Diffractive Holographic Neural System for Passive Optical Real-Time Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15364)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合衍射全息架构，将衍射光学神经网络（DONN）与全息干涉学习（HIBL）算子相结合，通过波前变换在无源光学介质中实现物理嵌入的推理，在MNIST上达到91.2%准确率，实现纳秒级延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能受限于电子存储器层级中数据传输的能量和延迟成本。光学系统提供了一种根本不同的计算范式：输入波前在结构化介质中传播时，衍射和干涉共同执行线性变换，其成本由波动物理而非时钟算术决定。现有光学机器学习文献在学习变换与物理实现之间存在持续差距。

Method: 提出混合衍射全息架构，将衍射光学神经网络（DONN）与全息干涉学习（HIBL）算子耦合。HIBL算子提供从数字优化相位分布到物理可实现、制造兼容干涉图案的形式化映射。将完整推理流程表达为编码、相位调制、自由空间传播和强度测量算子的组合，采用算子理论视角明确区分学习量、设计固定量以及通过光电检测引入的非线性。

Result: 在MNIST数据集的物理启发仿真中，三层系统（约25,000个相位元件）实现91.2%测试准确率，具有传播限制的纳秒级延迟。主要贡献不是性能声明，而是建立精确的计算框架：学习到的表示可以物理嵌入到结构化光学介质中。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了精确的计算框架，解决了光学机器学习文献中学习变换与物理实现之间的差距。通过将学习表示物理嵌入无源光学介质，推理由通过被动、制造物体的波前变换执行，而非顺序电子乘累加操作，为边缘智能提供了新的计算范式。

Abstract: Edge intelligence is constrained by the energy and latency costs of shuttling data through electronic memory hierarchies. Optical systems offer a fundamentally different computational regime: once an input wavefront is launched into a structured medium, propagation, diffraction, and interference jointly enact a linear transformation whose cost is determined by wave physics rather than by clocked arithmetic. This paper develops a rigorous systems-level treatment of that regime and introduces a hybrid diffractive holographic architecture for image classification. The proposed model couples a Diffractive Optical Neural Network (DONN) with a Holographic Interference-Based Learning (HIBL) operator a formal map from digitally optimized phase distributions to physically realizable, fabrication-compatible interference patterns embeddable in passive optical elements. We express the full inference pipeline as a composition of encoding, phase modulation, free-space propagation, and intensity measurement operators, making explicit which quantities are learned, which are fixed by design, and where nonlinearity enters through photodetection. This operator-theoretic view resolves a persistent gap in the optical-ML literature between learning a transformation and physically realizing it. In physics-informed simulation on MNIST, a three-layer system with approximately 25,000 phase elements achieves 91.2% test accuracy with propagation-limited nanosecond-scale latency. The primary contribution is not a performance claim but a precise computational framework: learned representations can be physically embedded into structured optical media so that inference is executed by wavefront transformation through a passive, fabricated object rather than by sequential electronic multiply accumulate operations.

</details>


### [99] [VeriCWEty: Embedding enabled Line-Level CWE Detection in Verilog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15375)
*Prithwish Basu Roy,Zeng Wang,Anatolii Chuvashlov,Weihua Xiao,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Ramesh Karri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于嵌入的RTL代码漏洞检测框架，能够以模块级和行级粒度检测并分类常见缺陷（CWE），在识别CWE-1244等漏洞时达到约89%的精确率，行级漏洞检测准确率达到96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）在RTL代码生成方面取得了显著进展，但生成的代码常包含容易被忽视的常见漏洞和弱点（CWE），攻击者可能利用这些弱点实施恶意行为。现有的RTL漏洞检测技术依赖基于规则的检查、形式化属性或粗粒度结构分析，无法捕捉语义漏洞或缺乏精确定位能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于嵌入的漏洞检测框架，能够在模块级和行级两种粒度上检测并分类RTL代码中的漏洞。

Result: 该方法在识别常见漏洞（如CWE-1244和CWE-1245）方面达到约89%的精确率，在行级漏洞检测方面达到96%的准确率。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入的检测框架有效填补了现有RTL漏洞检测技术的空白，实现了对语义漏洞的精确识别与定位，显著提升了RTL代码的安全性检测能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown significant improvement in RTL code generation. Despite the advances, the generated code is often riddled with common vulnerabilities and weaknesses (CWEs) that can slip by untrained eyes. Attackers can often exploit these weaknesses to fulfill their nefarious motives. Existing RTL bug-detection techniques rely on rule-based checks, formal properties, or coarse-grained structural analysis, which either fail to capture semantic vulnerabilities or lack precise localization. In our work, we bridge this gap by proposing an embedding-based bug-detection framework that detects and classifies bugs at both module and line-level granularity. Our method achieves about 89% precision in identifying common CWEs such as CWE-1244 and CWE-1245, and 96% accuracy in detecting line-level bugs.

</details>


### [100] [Fleet: Hierarchical Task-based Abstraction for Megakernels on Multi-Die GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15379)
*Sangeeta Chowdhary,Ryan Swann,Sean Siddens,Muhammad Osama,Stephen Neuendorffer,Alexandru Dutu,Karthik Sangaiah,Sandeepa Bhuyan,Samuel Bayliss,Ganesh Dasika*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Fleet多级任务模型，通过引入Chiplet-tasks抽象解决现代GPU小芯片设计与编程模型的不匹配问题，在Qwen3-8B上实现1.3-1.5倍延迟降低，L2命中率提升至61%，HBM流量减少37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU采用多小芯片设计，每个小芯片拥有私有缓存层次结构，但现有编程模型（CUDA/HIP）暴露的是扁平执行层次，无法表达小芯片级局部性或同步。这种不匹配导致内存受限工作负载（如LLM推理）中出现冗余内存流量和缓存利用率低下的问题。

Method: Fleet提出多级任务模型将计算映射到内存作用域，引入Chiplet-tasks新抽象将工作和数据绑定到小芯片并通过共享L2缓存实现协调。同时包含Wavefront级、CU级和设备级任务。实现为持久化内核运行时，采用每小芯片调度机制，允许小芯片内工作线程协作执行任务并协调缓存重用。

Result: 在AMD Instinct MI350平台上运行Qwen3-8B：批次大小1-8时解码延迟比vLLM降低1.3-1.5倍；批次大小32时L2命中率从12%提升至54%，批次大小64时从39%提升至61%；HBM流量减少高达37%；相比无小芯片感知的megakernel基线实现1.27-1.30倍加速。

Conclusion: Fleet成功弥合了小芯片GPU硬件层次结构与编程模型之间的鸿沟，通过Chiplet-tasks抽象实现了硬件感知的计算与数据映射，证明了针对小芯片架构的专用编程抽象对LLM推理性能优化的重要性。

Abstract: Modern GPUs adopt chiplet-based designs with multiple private cache hierarchies, but current programming models (CUDA/HIP) expose a flat execution hierarchy that cannot express chiplet-level locality or synchronization. This mismatch leads to redundant memory traffic and poor cache utilization in memory-bound workloads such as LLM inference.
  We present Fleet, a multi-level task model that maps computation to memory scopes. Fleet introduces Chiplet-tasks, a new abstraction that binds work and data to a chiplet and enables coordination through its shared L2 cache. Wavefront-level, CU-level, and device-level tasks align with existing abstractions, while Chiplet-tasks expose a previously unaddressed level of the hierarchy. Fleet is implemented as a persistent kernel runtime with per-chiplet scheduling, allowing workers within a chiplet to cooperatively execute tasks with coordinated cache reuse. On AMD Instinct MI350 with Qwen3-8B, Fleet achieves 1.3-1.5x lower decode latency than vLLM at batch sizes 1-8 through persistent kernel execution and per-chiplet scheduling. At larger batch sizes, cooperative weight tiling increases L2 hit rate (from 12% to 54% at batch size 32 and from 39% to 61% at batch size 64), reducing HBM traffic by up to 37% and delivering 1.27-1.30x speedup over a chiplet-unaware megakernel baseline.

</details>


### [101] [Exploring LLM-based Verilog Code Generation with Data-Efficient Fine-Tuning and Testbench Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15388)
*Mu-Chi Chen,Po-Hsuan Huang,Yu-Hung Kao,Yen-Fu Liu,Yu-Kai Hung,Cheng Liang,Shao-Chun Ho,Chia-Heng Tu,Shih-Hao Hung*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用多智能体模型自动生成测试平台的工作流，从而为规范到Verilog任务生成高质量的微调数据，在减少训练数据量的同时达到了最先进方法的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在代码生成方面取得了进展，但在硬件描述语言（HDL）领域的应用仍然受限，且针对该领域的训练数据与测试平台往往十分稀缺。

Method: 提出了一种基于多智能体模型的工作流，用于自动生成测试平台，进而构建高质量的微调数据集，并将其应用于规范到Verilog的代码生成任务。

Result: 在改进后的VerilogEval v2基准测试中，经该方法微调的模型在使用更少训练数据的情况下，取得了与最先进方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来基于大语言模型的HDL生成及自动化验证工作奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have improved code generation, but their use in hardware description languages is still limited. Moreover, training data and testbenches for these models are often scarce. This paper presents a workflow that uses multi-agent models to generate testbenches for high-quality fine-tuning data. By automating testbench creation, the fine-tuned model for the specification-to-Verilog task achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art methods on the refined VerilogEval v2 benchmark while using less training data. This study provides a basis for future work on LLM-based HDL generation and automated verification.

</details>


### [102] [EasyRider: Mitigating Power Transients in Datacenter-Scale Training Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15522)
*Dillon Jensen,Obi Nnorom,Grant Wilkins,Hugo Budd,Ram Rajagopal,Juan Rivas-Davila,Phil Levis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EasyRider，一种机柜级电源架构，利用被动元件和主动控制的辅助储能系统来缓解AI大模型训练中GPU负载剧变导致的电网功率波动问题，在无需修改训练框架的前提下保障电网安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI模型训练使用数千个GPU，在同步通信、启停和检查点期间，功耗会在毫秒级内从峰值骤降至空闲。这种剧烈且快速的负载波动会对电网基础设施造成威胁，引发陡峭的功率爬坡率、电压和频率偏移以及无功功率瞬变，可能损坏变压器、转换器和保护设备。

Method: 引入EasyRider机柜级电源架构，结合被动元件和主动控制的辅助储能系统来衰减机柜功率波动。该系统包含软件层面，持续监控储能系统以在频繁充放电循环中最大化其使用寿命。该方法无需对AI训练框架进行软件修改，也不浪费能量。

Result: 在一个400VDC额定原型系统上对EasyRider进行了评估，测试使用了公开的工作负载轨迹和自建的GPU测试平台。结果表明，EasyRider能够有效过滤机柜功率变化，使其符合电网安全要求，并在异构功率水平和不同工作负载功率特征下均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: EasyRider成功解决了AI训练负载剧变带来的电网安全隐患，能够在不修改现有AI软件栈的情况下，将功率波动限制在电网安全要求范围内，同时兼顾了储能系统的使用寿命，为大规模AI数据中心的电网接入提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Large-scale AI model training workloads use thousands of GPUs operating in tightly synchronized loops. During synchronous communication, start-up, shut-down, and checkpointing, GPU power consumption can swing from peak to idle within milliseconds. These large and rapid load swings endanger grid infrastructure as they induce steep power ramp rates, voltage and frequency shifts, and reactive power transients that can damage transformers, converters, and protection equipment. To solve this problem, we introduce EasyRider, a power architecture to mitigate power fluctuations at the rack level. EasyRider uses passive components and actively-controlled auxiliary energy storage to attenuate rack power swings. A software system continually monitors the energy storage system to maximize its lifetime in the presence of frequent charge/discharge cycles. EasyRider filters rack power variations to be within grid safety requirements without requiring software modifications to AI training frameworks or wasting energy. We evaluate EasyRider on a 400VDC-rated prototype system against published workload traces and our own GPU testbed, demonstrating its effectiveness across heterogeneous power levels and workload power profiles.

</details>


### [103] [Spec2Cov: An Agentic Framework for Code Coverage Closure of Digital Hardware Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15606)
*Sean Lowe,Elias Hilaneh,Alma Babbit,Nakul Gopalan,Vidya Chhabria,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了Spec2Cov框架，利用LLM与硬件模拟器的协同交互，自动从设计规范迭代生成测试激励，从而加速硬件验证中的覆盖率闭合过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件验证是设计流程中最具挑战性的阶段之一，传统的覆盖率闭合过程依赖人工迭代的约束随机测试与定向测试，耗时费力。虽然近期LLM在代码生成方面表现出潜力，但如何将其有效集成以自动化硬件验证流程仍需探索。

Method: 提出了Spec2Cov智能体框架，协调LLM与硬件模拟器之间的交互。该框架直接从设计规范自动生成测试激励，管理编译和仿真错误，解析覆盖率报告，并将结果反馈给模型进行迭代优化。同时引入了无需额外微调即可提升框架有效性的特性。

Result: 在包括CVDP基准测试套件在内的26个不同规模和复杂度的设计上进行评估，Spec2Cov在简单设计上实现了100%的覆盖率，在更复杂的设计上达到了最高49%的覆盖率，展现了良好的性能表现。

Conclusion: Spec2Cov框架证明了利用LLM智能体工作流自动化硬件验证任务的可行性，能够有效加速覆盖率闭合过程，为硬件设计验证提供了新的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Hardware verification is one of the most challenging stages of the hardware design process, requiring significant time and resources to ensure a design is fully validated and production-ready. Verification teams aim to maximize design coverage while ensuring correct behavior and alignment with the specification. Coverage closure, which relies on iterative constrained-random and directed testing, is still largely manual and therefore slow and labor-intensive. Recent advances show that the code generation capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) can be integrated with external tools to build agentic workflows that autonomously perform hardware design and verification tasks. In this work, we introduce Spec2Cov, an agentic framework that automatically and iteratively generates test stimulus directly from design specifications to accelerate coverage closure. Spec2Cov coordinates interactions between an LLM and a hardware simulator, managing compilation and simulation errors, parsing coverage reports, and feeding results back to the model for refinement. We present features that improve Spec2Cov's effectiveness without additional fine-tuning and evaluate their impact. Across 26 designs of varying size and complexity, including problems from the CVDP benchmark suite, Spec2Cov demonstrates promising performance, achieving 100% coverage on simpler designs and up to 49% on more complex designs.

</details>


### [104] [Overmind NSA: A Unified Neuro-Symbolic Computing Architecture with Approximate Nonlinear Activations and Preemptive Memory Bypass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15623)
*Weilun Wang,Zirui Wang,Wantong Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Overmind，一种针对神经符号AI计算瓶颈的统一架构，通过Padé近似、抢占式内存旁路和软件栈优化，在保持精度的同时显著提升了性能和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号AI结合了感知与结构化推理，在LLM、科学发现等领域应用广泛，但其部署受限于高内存需求、多样化计算模式及复杂硬件要求。现有硬件平台面临片上内存开销大、流水线停顿频繁、I/O带宽有限及非线性运算处理低效等问题。

Method: 提出Overmind统一神经符号架构，采用三层跨层优化：1) 基于Padé近似的通用非线性函数处理；2) 抢占式内存旁路消除昂贵的片上缓存；3) 完整软件栈优化模型部署；同时支持通过重配置Padé阶数实现精度-性能的自适应调节。

Result: Overmind在混合神经符号工作负载下实现了8.1 TOPS/W的能效和410 GOPS的吞吐量，模型精度损失极小。与现有方案相比，在硬件资源显著减少的情况下提升了性能和效率。

Conclusion: Overmind有效解决了神经符号AI部署中的核心计算瓶颈，为该领域提供了一种高效、低资源消耗的硬件解决方案，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic AI is gaining traction in domains such as large language models, scientific discovery, and autonomous systems due to its ability to combine perception with structured reasoning. However, its deployment is often constrained by high memory demands, diverse computation patterns, and complex hardware requirements. Existing hardware platforms struggle with large on-chip memory overheads, frequent pipeline stalls, limited I/O bandwidth, and inefficient handling of nonlinear operations. To address these key computational bottlenecks, we propose Overmind, a unified neuro-symbolic architecture with cross-layer optimizations. Overmind tackles these core bottlenecks through Padé approximations for universal nonlinear functions, preemptive memory bypass that eliminates costly on-chip caches, and a complete software stack that optimizes model deployment. By reconfiguring the Padé orders for approximating nonlinear functions, we also demonstrate adaptive accuracy-performance scaling. Overmind achieves an energy efficiency of 8.1 TOPS/W and a throughput of 410 GOPS for mixed neuro-symbolic workloads with minimal model accuracy loss. Compared to existing solutions, Overmind improves performance and efficiency with significantly fewer hardware resources.

</details>


### [105] [HYPERHEURIST: A Simulated Annealing-Based Control Framework for LLM-Driven Code Generation in Optimized Hardware Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15642)
*Shiva Ahir,Prajna Bhat,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HYPERHEURIST框架，一种基于模拟退火的控制方法，通过分阶段筛选和优化LLM生成的RTL设计，解决了单次生成在功能正确性和功耗效率方面的不足，实现了更稳定的PPA优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLM）在生成RTL硬件设计方面展现出潜力，能够快速提出不同的架构实现，但单次生成（single-shot generation）难以同时保证设计的功能正确性和功耗效率（power-efficient），无法稳定地生成高质量的设计。

Method: 提出HYPERHEURIST框架，采用基于模拟退火（simulated annealing）的控制策略，将LLM生成的RTL视为中间候选而非最终设计。该方法分为两个阶段：第一阶段通过编译、结构检查和仿真过滤RTL候选，识别出功能正确的设计；第二阶段仅对通过验证的设计进行Power-Performance-Area（PPA）优化。

Result: 在八个RTL基准测试上的评估结果表明，这种分阶段的方法比单次LLM生成的RTL能够产生更加稳定和可重复的优化行为，同时保证了功能正确性和PPA性能。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM生成的RTL作为中间候选并进行分阶段验证与优化，HYPERHEURIST框架有效地解决了单次LLM生成的不稳定性问题，为RTL设计自动化提供了一种更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising progress for generating Register Transfer Level (RTL) hardware designs, largely because they can rapidly propose alternative architectural realizations. However, single-shot LLM generation struggles to consistently produce designs that are both functionally correct and power-efficient. This paper proposes HYPERHEURIST, a simulated annealing-based control framework that treats LLM-generated RTL as intermediate candidates rather than final designs. The suggested system not only focuses on functionality correctness but also on Power-Performance-Area (PPA) optimization. In the first phase, RTL candidates are filtered through compilation, structural checks, and simulation to identify functionally valid designs. PPA optimization is restricted to RTL designs that have already passed compilation and simulation. Evaluated across eight RTL benchmarks, this staged approach yields more stable and repeatable optimization behavior than single-pass LLM-generated RTL.

</details>


### [106] [Understanding Inference-Time Token Allocation and Coverage Limits in Agentic Hardware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15657)
*Vihaan Patel,Vidya Chhabria,Aman Arora*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对基于LLM的硬件验证覆盖率闭合进行了实证研究，提出了覆盖漏洞分类法，并证明领域专业化系统相比通用基线可减少4-13倍token使用，同时实现95-99%覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖率闭合是硬件验证中最耗时的阶段。虽然近期基于LLM的编码代理为自动化激励生成提供了新途径，但现有流程未能系统分析难以闭合的覆盖漏洞，也未深入研究推理时的计算分配。因此，LLM驱动方法在大规模设计中的效率极限和失效模式仍未被充分理解。

Method: 采用双层代理框架进行实证研究，包含基础Codex代理和增强型领域专用LangGraph系统。该方法建立了覆盖漏洞分类法（方法界限与推理前沿），并对系统进行仪器化以追踪六类token使用情况，包括系统提示、设计理解、激励生成、覆盖率反馈、错误恢复和代理开销。

Result: 领域专业化将token分配转向覆盖率导向的推理并提高效率。增强系统在多个设计上实现了相当或更高的覆盖率（95-99%），同时token使用量减少4-13倍，收敛速度比通用基线快2-4倍。研究揭示了纯LLM方法的基本限制，包括集成绑定硬件、不可行边界和死代码等方法界限，以及协议排序、多模块流水线预热、窄时序条件等推理前沿。

Conclusion: 研究结果系统刻画了基于LLM的覆盖率闭合的局限性，为基准测试设计、人工升级策略提供了依据，并为硬件验证中的配置驱动代理设计提供了指导，有助于更有效地将LLM技术应用于硬件验证领域。

Abstract: Coverage closure is the most time-consuming phase of hardware verification, and recent large language model (LLM)-based coding agents offer a promising approach to automated stimulus generation. However, prior LLM-based flows do not systematically analyze which coverage holes remain difficult to close or how inference-time computation is allocated during agentic verification. As a result, the efficiency limits and failure modes of LLM-based coverage closure remain poorly understood, particularly for large designs. We present an empirical study using a two-tier agentic framework comprising a base Codex agent and an enhanced domain-specialized LangGraph system. Our framework enables a taxonomy of coverage holes: methodology-bound ceilings (integration tied-off hardware, infeasible boundaries, dead code) and reasoning frontiers (protocol sequencing, multi-module pipeline warm-up, narrow timing conditions), exposing fundamental limits of purely LLM-driven approaches. We further instrument the system to track token usage across six categories, including system prompt, design comprehension, stimulus generation, coverage feedback, error recovery, and agentic overhead. We show that domain specialization shifts token allocation toward coverage-directed reasoning and improves efficiency. Across designs, the enhanced system achieves comparable or higher coverage (95-99%) while using 4-13x fewer tokens and converging to coverage targets 2-4x faster than a general-purpose baseline. Our results characterize the limits of LLM-based coverage closure, inform benchmark design and human escalation strategies, and guide profile-driven agent design for hardware verification.

</details>


### [107] [CIMple: Standard-cell SRAM-based CIM with LUT-based split softmax for attention acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15944)
*Bas Ahn,Xingjian Tao,Manil Dev Gomongy,Marc Geilen,Henk Corporaal*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CIMple的全数字SRAM存内计算架构，专门用于加速Transformer模型中的自注意力机制，以解决边缘设备上部署大语言模型面临的资源限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（如LLaMA和DeepSeek）虽然性能优异，但参数量巨大，在资源受限的边缘设备上部署面临挑战。现有的存内计算（CIM）架构虽然能减少数据移动，但仅支持静态乘累加（MAC）运算，限制了其对非线性操作和不同Transformer模型的支持能力。

Method: 提出CIMple加速器，采用基于标准单元SRAM的全数字CIM架构。主要技术包括：1）采用双存储体CIM架构，支持8位并行权重馈送；2）基于查找表（LUT）的定点数实现方案，在保证精度的同时降低延迟；3）在28nm工艺下实现32kb的CIM自注意力加速器原型。

Result: 在28nm工艺下实现的32kb CIM自注意力加速器，在0.85V电压下达到26.1 TOPS/W的能效，在1.2V电压下达到2.31 TOPS/mm²的面积效率，均采用INT8精度。基于LUT的定点实现在最小化精度损失的同时显著降低了延迟。

Conclusion: CIMple成功克服了现有CIM架构的局限性，为Transformer模型在边缘设备上的高效部署提供了一种可配置、高性能的解决方案，通过支持非线性操作实现了对自注意力机制的完整加速。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) such as LLaMA and DeepSeek, are built on transformer architectures, which have become a standard model for achieving state-of-the-art performance in natural language processing tasks. Recently, there has been growing interest in deploying LLMs on edge devices. Although smaller LLM models are being proposed, they often still contain billions of parameters. Since edge devices are limited in their resources this poses a significant challenge for edge deployment. Compute-in-memory (CIM) is a promising architecture that addresses this by reducing data movement through the integration of computational logic directly into memory. However, existing CIM architectures support only static Multiply-Accumulate (MAC) operations which limit their configurability in supporting nonlinear operations and various types of transformer models. This paper presents a fully digital standard-cell SRAM-based CIM architecture accelerator for self-attention, called CIMple, designed to overcome these limitations, inside transformer models. The key contributions of CIMple are: 1) A novel dual-banked CIM-based fully digital self-attention accelerator using 8-bit parallel weight feeding. 2) A look-up-table (LUT) based fixed-point implementation reducing latency with minimal accuracy degradation. 3) A performance evaluation of a 32kb CIM-based self-attention accelerator implemented in 28nm, which achieves 26.1 TOPS/W at 0.85V and 2.31 TOPS/mm$^2$ at 1.2V, both with INT8 precision.

</details>


### [108] [MemExplorer: Navigating the Heterogeneous Memory Design Space for Agentic Inference NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16007)
*Haoran Wu,Zeyu Cao,Yao Lai,Binglei Lou,Jiayi Nie,Can Xiao,Timi Adeniran,Przemyslaw Forys,Kauser Johar,Catriona Wright,Junyi Liu,Kai Shi,Nicholas D. Lane,Rika Antonova,Jianyi Cheng,Timothy Jones,Aaron Zhao,Robert Mullins*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MemExplorer，一种针对异构NPU系统的内存系统综合器，可自动确定高效的异构内存配置与NPU设计选择，在智能体LLM负载下实现能效最高提升3.23倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的智能体LLM工作负载对内存容量和带宽需求快速增长，预填充与解码阶段需求各异；硬件加速器走向异构互联系统，内存技术选项（SRAM、HBM、LPDDR、GDDR、HBF等）日益多样。工作负载特性、NPU设计与内存系统之间的交互关系亟待探索。

Method: 提出MemExplorer内存系统综合器，提供统一抽象对多层级（片上/片外）多样化内存技术建模，自动联合优化异构内存系统配置与NPU设计参数（如矩阵引擎规模），以平衡多设备NPU系统中预填充与解码设备的吞吐量与功耗。

Result: 在相同功耗预算下，预填充场景能效较基线NPU提升2.3倍，较H100提升3.23倍；在等效性能目标下，解码场景功耗效率较基线NPU提升1.93倍，较H100提升2.72倍。

Conclusion: MemExplorer有效解决了异构NPU系统内存架构设计空间探索问题，实现了预填充与解码阶段的能效权衡，为下一代推理加速器的内存系统设计提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Emerging agentic LLM workloads are driving rapidly growing demand on both memory capacity and bandwidth, with different phases of inference (e.g., prefill and decode) imposing distinct requirements. Industry is responding by composing heterogeneous accelerators into single interconnected systems, as exemplified by NVIDIA's Vera Rubin platform, where each device brings its own memory architecture.
  This heterogeneity is further compounded by a widening landscape of available memory technologies: high-density on-chip SRAM, HBM, LPDDR, GDDR, and emerging options such as high-bandwidth flash (HBF), each offering different capacity, bandwidth, and power trade-offs.
  Identifying the right memory architecture for next-generation inference accelerators requires navigating a vast and rapidly evolving design space, in which the interplay between workload characteristics, NPU design dimensions, and memory system design remains largely underexplored.
  To address this challenge, we present MemExplorer, a new memory system synthesizer for heterogeneous NPU systems. MemExplorer provides a unified abstraction for modeling diverse memory technologies across different hierarchy levels (e.g., on-chip and off-chip) and automatically determines an efficient heterogeneous memory system together with NPU design choices (e.g., matrix engine size) to balance throughput and power between prefilling and decoding devices in a multi-device NPU system.
  Experimental results show that, under the same power budget for agentic workloads, MemExplorer achieves up to 2.3x higher energy efficiency than the baseline NPU and 3.23x higher than H100 in the prefill-only setting. Under equivalent performance targets in the decode setting, it further delivers up to 1.93x and 2.72x higher power efficiency over the baseline NPU and H100, respectively.

</details>


### [109] [Co-Design of CNN Accelerators for TinyML using Approximate Matrix Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16113)
*José Juan Hernández Morales,Georgios Mentzos,Frank Hannig,Konstantinos Balaskas,Georgios Zervakis,Jörg Henkel,Jürgen Teich*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于遗传算法的框架，通过近似矩阵分解优化CNN在FPGA上的硬件实现，在无需重训练的情况下，平均降低33%的推理延迟，准确率损失仅1.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TinyML领域需要在严格资源约束下进行本地推理，但超低资源设备会导致推理延迟增加，影响时延关键型应用。同时，TinyML应用常涉及敏感数据，传统依赖训练样本的延迟优化方法不可行，亟需无需访问训练数据、可直接应用于预训练模型的优化方法。虽然用移位和加法替代乘法可降低延迟，但现有的训练后二次幂方法很少且会导致不可接受的精度损失。

Method: 提出一个框架，将近似矩阵分解应用于给定的CNN，以在严格约束下优化硬件实现，无需重训练或微调步骤。该框架利用遗传算法探索不同的矩阵分解方式，并针对FPGA目标设计无乘法器的CNN加速器。

Result: 在多个TinyML基准测试上的综合评估表明，该框架能有效生成满足严格精度和资源约束的延迟优化实现。与典型的基于脉动阵列的FPGA加速器相比，平均降低33%的延迟，平均准确率损失仅为1.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了TinyML场景下无需训练数据的硬件优化难题，在保持可接受精度损失的前提下显著降低了推理延迟，为资源受限设备上的CNN部署提供了一种有效的训练后优化解决方案。

Abstract: The paradigm shift towards local and on-device inference under stringent resource constraints is represented by the tiny machine learning (TinyML) domain. The primary goal of \gls{tml} is to integrate intelligence into tiny, low-cost devices under strict resource, energy, and latency constraints. However, the ultra-resource-constrained nature of these devices can lead to increased inference execution time, which can be detrimental in latency critical applications. At the same time, TinyML applications are often associated with sensitive data. As such, latency optimization approaches that rely on training samples are infeasible when such data is unavailable, proprietary, or sensitive, highlighting a pressing need for optimization approaches that do not require access to the training dataset and can be applied directly to pre-trained models. Replacing costly multiplications with more hardware-efficient operations, such as shifts and additions, has been proposed as an effective method for reducing inference latency. However, post-training power-of-two (Po2) approaches are scarce and, in many cases, lead to unacceptable accuracy loss.
  In this work, we propose a framework that applies approximate matrix decomposition to a given CNN in order to optimize hardware implementations subject to strict constraints and without any need of re-training or fine-tuning steps. The genetic algorithm-driven framework explores different matrix decompositions and resulting multiplier-less CNN accelerator designs for FPGA targets. A comprehensive evaluation of different TinyML benchmarks demonstrates our framework's efficacy in generating latency-optimized implementations that satisfy strict accuracy and resource constraints, achieving an average 33% latency improvement with an average accuracy loss of 1.3% compared to typical systolic array-based FPGA accelerators.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [DataCenterGym: A Physics-Grounded Simulator for Multi-Objective Data Center Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15594)
*Nilavra Pathak,Samadrita Biswas,Nirmalya Roy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一个名为DataCenterGym的物理仿真环境，用于地理分布式数据中心作业调度，并开发了分层模型预测控制(H-MPC)调度算法以优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据中心在跨地理分布式站点调度异构工作负载时，计算利用率、热量产生、冷却需求和能耗之间存在紧密耦合关系。然而，现有调度器大多将这些效应抽象化并独立处理，忽略了它们之间的相互依赖性，从而导致调度性能不佳。

Method: 开发了DataCenterGym，一个基于物理的仿真环境，集成了计算排队、建筑热动力学、局部暖通空调(HVAC)行为以及温度相关的服务降级，并提供与Gymnasium兼容的接口。同时提出了一种分层模型预测控制(H-MPC)调度算法，在分布式作业放置中显式考虑热力学和功率动态。

Result: 通过正常运行和工作负载敏感性实验验证，H-MPC算法相比基线调度器显著提升了调度性能，证明了在调度决策中显式考虑热力学和功率动态的有效性。

Conclusion: DataCenterGym为未来数据中心调度研究提供了可重用的测试平台，H-MPC方法展示了将计算、热力和电力系统联合优化的潜力，为跨学科数据中心管理提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Modern datacenters schedule heterogeneous workloads across geo-distributed sites with diverse compute capacities, electricity prices, and thermal conditions. Compute utilization, heat generation, cooling demand, and energy consumption are tightly coupled, yet most existing schedulers abstract these effects and treat them independently.
  We present \textit{DataCenterGym}, a physics-grounded simulation environment for job scheduling in geo-distributed data centers, designed as a reusable testbed for future research. The simulator integrates compute queueing, building thermal dynamics, localized HVAC behavior, and temperature-dependent service degradation within a Gymnasium-compatible interface. We also develop a Hierarchical Model Predictive Control (H-MPC) scheduling algorithm that performs distributed job placement while explicitly accounting for thermal and power dynamics. Through experiments on nominal operation and workload sensitivity, we demonstrate how H-MPC improves scheduling performance relative to baseline schedulers.

</details>


### [111] [BlockRaFT: A Distributed Framework for Fault-Tolerant and Scalable Blockchain Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15731)
*Manaswini Piduguralla,Souvik Sarkar,Arunmoezhi Ramachandran,Sathya Peri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了BlockRaFT，一个基于RAFT共识协议的崩溃容忍分布式框架，通过将区块链节点任务按有状态/无状态分区处理，并引入并发Merkle树优化，显著提升了区块链节点的可扩展性、可用性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链技术在互不信任方之间通过分布式账本提供透明性，但其可扩展性和可用性仍是限制其广泛采用的关键瓶颈；同时，区块链节点日益增加的复杂性迫切需要高吞吐量和健壮的容错机制以确保平稳运行。

Method: 提出BlockRaFT框架，在系统集群内利用RAFT共识协议选举领导者，由领导者协调并分发工作负载；将区块链节点任务划分为无状态与有状态两类，无状态操作集中于领导者处理，有状态操作在集群中复制与协调以确保一致性与容错；引入并发Merkle树优化，将智能合约执行与树更新解耦以降低开销。设计理念是利用成熟的分布式计算原则并针对区块链领域进行定制，而非重新发明。

Result: 评估结果表明，这种分布式节点内架构在可扩展性、可用性和性能方面相比传统单节点执行模型具有可衡量的提升；并发Merkle树优化显著减少了区块链系统中的性能开销。

Conclusion: BlockRaFT证明了通过将区块链节点任务按状态特征分区并结合RAFT共识与并发优化，能够有效解决区块链节点的可扩展性与可靠性问题，为区块链系统提供了一种高效、健壮的分布式执行模型。

Abstract: Blockchain technology enhances transparency by maintaining a distributed ledger among mutually untrusting parties. Despite its advantages, scalability and availability remain critical bottlenecks that hinder widespread adoption. The increasing complexity of blockchain nodes further necessitates robust fault tolerance and high throughput to ensure seamless operations.
  We present BlockRaFT, a crash-tolerant distributed framework designed to improve both the scalability and reliability of blockchain node operations. BlockRaFT framework utilizes RAFT consensus protocol to elect a leader within a cluster of systems. The elected leader coordinates and distributes workloads across follower nodes, thereby optimizing resource utilization and work load balancing. We analyzed the tasks performed by blockchain nodes and partition them according to their stateful and stateless characteristics. Stateless operations are centralized at the leader, while stateful operations are replicated and coordinated across the cluster to ensure consistency and fault tolerance. We evaluate whether this distributed intra-node architecture provides measurable benefits over traditional single-node execution models in terms of scalability, availability, and performance.
  Additionally, we introduce a concurrent Merkle tree optimization that decouples smart contract execution from tree updates, significantly reducing one of the significant performance overheads in blockchain systems. Our design philosophy is rooted in utilizing the well-established principles of distributed computing and customizing them for the blockchain domain rather than reinventing them.

</details>


### [112] [Accuracy Is Speed: Towards Long-Context-Aware Routing for Distributed LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15732)
*Takeshi Yoshimura,Valentijn Dymphnus van de Beek,Tatsuhiro Chiba*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出在长上下文分布式LLM服务中，准确率通过重试机制直接影响总体延迟。作者引入TTCA（获得正确答案所需时间）作为新指标，并提出LAAR路由机制以优化该指标，主张将准确率作为系统设计的首要目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
