Motivation: 现有的分布式LLM服务系统主要优化单次请求延迟和吞吐量，但在长上下文工作负载下，推理准确率波动变大。当错误响应触发重试时，准确率会转化为用户可见的累积延迟，而传统的单次延迟指标无法捕捉这一现象。

Method: 提出Time-to-Correct-Answer (TTCA)指标来衡量获得首个正确响应所需的实际时间。通过测量研究分析提示词特征（如长度和语言）如何放大准确率方差并导致TTCA增加。设计Lightweight Accuracy-Aware Routing (LAAR)，一种基于能力的路由方案来降低TTCA。

Result: 测量研究表明，提示词长度和语言等特征会放大准确率方差，从而增加TTCA。LAAR路由设计能够有效降低TTCA。实验结果验证了在长上下文服务中考虑准确率的重要性。

Conclusion: 在长上下文分布式LLM服务中，准确率应被视为一等系统目标。由于重试机制的存在，准确率本质上转化为速度，系统设计需要通过感知准确率的路由等机制来优化端到端的用户体验。

Abstract: Distributed LLM serving systems optimize per-request latency and throughput. However, under long-context workloads, inference accuracy becomes more variable. When incorrect responses trigger retries, accuracy directly translates into cumulative user-visible delay that is not captured by single-shot latency metrics.
  In this work, we argue that under long-context serving, \textbf{accuracy becomes speed} through retry dynamics. We introduce \textit{Time-to-Correct-Answer (TTCA)}, a metric that measures the wall-clock time required to obtain the first correct response. Our measurement study shows that prompt characteristics such as length and language amplify accuracy variance, which inflates TTCA. We demonstrate \textit{Lightweight Accuracy-Aware Routing (LAAR)}, a capability-based routing design that reduces TTCA. Our results suggest that in long-context distributed serving, accuracy should be treated as a first-class systems objective.

</details>


### [113] [cuNNQS-SCI: A Fully GPU-Accelerated Framework for High-Performance Configuration Interaction Selection withNeural Network QQantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15768)
*Daran Sun,Bowen Kan,Haoquan Long,Hairui Zhao,Haoxu Li,Yicheng Liu,Pengyu Zhou,Ankang Feng,Wenjing Huang,Yida Gu,Zhenyu Li,Honghui Shang,Yunquan Zhang,Dingwen Tao,Ninghui Sun,Guangming Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了cuNNQS-SCI，一个全GPU加速的SCI框架。通过分布式去重算法、细粒度CUDA内核以及以GPU内存为中心的运行时设计，解决了原有混合CPU-GPU架构的扩展性瓶颈，在64 GPU集群上实现了2.32倍加速并保持90%以上的并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NNQS-SCI方法是求解多体系统薛定谔方程的最先进技术，但其混合CPU-GPU架构存在严重的扩展性瓶颈：集中式CPU全局去重导致通信瓶颈，主机端的耦合组态生成带来巨大的计算开销，限制了其在更大系统上的应用。

Method: 提出了cuNNQS-SCI全GPU加速框架，包含三项核心技术：(1) 分布式负载均衡的全局去重算法，最小化冗余和通信开销；(2) 专用的细粒度CUDA内核用于精确耦合组态生成；(3) 以GPU内存为中心的运行时，包含GPU端池化、流式小批处理和重叠卸载技术，突破单GPU内存限制。

Result: 在配备64个NVIDIA A100 GPU的集群上，cuNNQS-SCI相比高度优化的NNQS-SCI基线实现了最高2.32倍的端到端加速，同时保持相同的化学精度。强扩展性测试中保持超过90%的并行效率，能够处理更大规模的组态空间。

Conclusion: cuNNQS-SCI成功突破了NNQS-SCI方法的扩展性瓶颈，将性能瓶颈从主机端限制转移回设备端推理，根本上扩大了可解决问题的规模，为更大尺度的量子多体系统计算提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: AI-driven methods have demonstrated considerable success in tackling the central challenge of accurately solving the Schrödinger equation for complex many-body systems. Among neural network quantum state (NNQS) approaches, the NNQS-SCI (Selected Configuration Interaction) method stands out as a state-of-the-art technique, recognized for its high accuracy and scalability. However, its application to larger systems is severely constrained by a hybrid CPU-GPU architecture. Specifically, centralized CPU-based global de-duplication creates a severe scalability barrier due to communication bottlenecks, while host-resident coupled-configuration generation induces prohibitive computational overheads. We introduce cuNNQS-SCI, a fully GPU-accelerated SCI framework designed to overcome these bottlenecks. cuNNQS-SCI first integrates a distributed, load-balanced global de-duplication algorithm to minimize redundancy and communication overhead at scale. To address compute limitations, it employs specialized, fine-grained CUDA kernels for exact coupled configuration generation. Finally, to break the single-GPU memory barrier exposed by this full acceleration, it incorporates a GPU memory-centric runtime featuring GPU-side pooling, streaming mini-batches, and overlapped offloading. This design enables much larger configuration spaces and shifts the bottleneck from host-side limitations back to on-device inference. Our evaluation demonstrates that cuNNQS-SCI fundamentally expands the scale of solvable problems. On an NVIDIA A100 cluster with 64 GPUs, cuNNQS-SCI achieves up to 2.32X end-to-end speedup over the highly-optimized NNQS-SCI baseline while preserving the same chemical accuracy. Furthermore, it demonstrates excellent distributed performance, maintaining over 90% parallel efficiency in strong scaling tests.

</details>


### [114] [CroSatFL: Energy-Efficient Federated Learning with Cross-Aggregation for Satellite Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15779)
*Nan Yang,Bahman Javadi,Rodrigo Neves Calheiros,David Boland,Philip Leong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出CroSatFL，一种面向LEO巨型星座的可持续星上分层联邦学习框架，通过将迭代过程转移至星间激光链路，显著降低地面站通信能耗（约6倍），同时保持训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道（LEO）巨型星座中的卫星边缘计算为联邦学习（FL）提供了新场景，但该环境受动态星间连接、异构星载计算硬件和严格功率预算限制，面临根本性的能源约束，需要设计适应这些特性的节能FL框架。

Method: 提出CroSatFL星上分层联邦学习框架，将所有本地训练和中间聚合在轨完成，仅保留初始化和最终模型收集两次地面站通信。该框架集成三种节能机制：1）StarMask构建与异构CPU/GPU能力匹配的星间激光链路（LISL）可行簇；2）Skip-One通过每簇最多跳过一个慢速客户端缓解瞬时落后者，降低轮次能耗和延迟；3）random-k交叉聚合实现轻量级拓扑感知跨簇混合。

Result: 基于Walker-Delta星座的真实端到端能耗模型实验表明，CroSatFL将地面站通信次数减少超过两个数量级，地面站传输能耗降低约6倍，同时实现具有竞争力的准确率和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CroSatFL有效解决了LEO卫星联邦学习中的能源约束问题，通过星上分层聚合和三种节能机制显著降低了通信能耗，为可持续的星上联邦学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Low Earth Orbit (LEO) mega-constellations extend the cloud-to-edge continuum into space, enabling satellite edge computing. However, Federated Learning (FL) in this environment is fundamentally energy-constrained due to dynamic inter-satellite connectivity, heterogeneous onboard computing hardware, and strict power budgets. We propose CroSatFL, a sustainable on-orbit hierarchical FL framework that reduces end-to-end energy across computation and communication while maintaining strong training performance under realistic LEO dynamics. CroSatFL keeps the ground station (GS) off the iterative loop by performing all local training and intermediate aggregations on orbit, requiring only two GS communication phases: one for initialization and one for final model collection. This sharply reduces repeated use of bandwidth-limited and energy-expensive GS links and shifts iterative exchanges to laser inter-satellite links (LISLs). CroSatFL integrates three energy-aware mechanisms: StarMask forms LISL-feasible clusters that align data volume with heterogeneous CPU/GPU capability, Skip-One mitigates transient stragglers by skipping at most one slow client per cluster to lower round energy and latency while preserving long-term fairness, and random-k cross-aggregation enables lightweight topology-aware cross-cluster mixing without extending round duration. Using an end-to-end energy model with a realistic Walker-Delta constellation, we show that CroSatFL reduces GS communication count by over two orders of magnitude and GS transmission energy by about 6x relative to GS-centric and on-orbit baselines, while achieving competitive accuracy and faster convergence.

</details>


### [115] [Breaking the Training Barrier of Billion-Parameter Universal Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15821)
*Yuanchang Zhou,Hongyu Wang,Yiming Du,Yan Wang,Mingzhen Li,Siyu Hu,Xiangyu Zhang,Weijian Liu,Chen Wang,Zhuoqiang Guo,Long Wang,Jingde Bu,Yutong Lu,Guangming Tan,Weile Jia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了MatRIS-MoE（基于不变架构的十亿参数混合专家模型）和Janus（首个面向通用机器学习原子间势的高维分布式训练框架），在两个Exascale超算上实现了1.2/1.0 EFLOPS的峰值性能，将十亿参数模型的训练时间从数周压缩至数小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势（uMLIPs）作为量子精度物理模拟的基础模型，其训练需要二阶导数且缺乏相应的并行训练框架；同时，扩展到十亿参数规模时，计算和通信开销呈爆炸式增长，使得训练面临巨大挑战。

Method: 构建了基于不变架构的十亿参数混合专家模型MatRIS-MoE，并开发了名为Janus的高维分布式训练框架，该框架针对uMLIPs进行了硬件感知优化，支持二阶导数的并行计算。

Result: 在两台Exascale超算上部署后，代码在单精度下达到1.2/1.0 EFLOPS的峰值性能（达到理论峰值的24%/35.5%），并行效率超过90%，成功将十亿参数uMLIPs的训练时间从数周压缩至数小时。

Conclusion: 该工作为Exascale级别的AI-for-Science（AI4S）基础模型设立了新的标杆，并为快速科学发现提供了关键基础设施。

Abstract: Universal Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials (uMLIPs), pre-trained on massively diverse datasets encompassing inorganic materials and organic molecules across the entire periodic table, serve as foundational models for quantum-accurate physical simulations. However, uMLIP training requires second-order derivatives, which lack corresponding parallel training frameworks; moreover, scaling to the billion-parameter regime causes explosive growth in computation and communication overhead, making its training a tremendous challenge. We introduce MatRIS-MoE, a billion-parameter Mixture-of-Experts model built upon invariant architecture, and {Janus}, a pioneering high-dimensional distributed training framework for uMLIPs with hardware-aware optimizations. Deployed across two Exascale supercomputers, our code attains a peak performance of 1.2/1.0 EFLOPS (24\%/{35.5\%} of theoretical peak) in single precision at over 90\% parallel efficiency, compressing the training of billion-parameter uMLIPs from weeks to hours. This work establishes a new high-water mark for AI-for-Science (AI4S) foundation models at Exascale and provides essential infrastructure for rapid scientific discovery.

</details>


### [116] [New Kids: An Architecture and Performance Investigation of Second-Generation Serverless Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15916)
*Trever Schirmer,Aris Wiegand,Lucca di Benedetto,Linus Gustafsson,Natalie Carl,Tobias Pfandzelter,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了无服务器计算平台的演进，识别出基于轻量级隔离和边缘部署的第二代平台，其延迟从约40ms降至10ms，并通过对7个平台进行超过3800万次函数调用的微基准测试揭示了架构与性能特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无服务器计算在工业界和学术界的广泛应用，理解底层平台的机制至关重要。由于无服务器技术已发展超过十年，出现了架构迥异的不同平台，因此有必要系统性地研究这些平台的演进和性能差异。

Method: 收集并分析所有公开可用的平台信息，详细剖析七个无服务器平台的底层架构；设计并执行微基准测试，累计发起超过3800万次函数调用，深入评估平台性能表现。

Result: 识别出第二代无服务器平台，其采用轻量级隔离和边缘部署架构；相比第一代基于容器化集中式执行的平台，热请求延迟从约40ms降低至约10ms，冷启动问题基本消除，但执行环境受到一定限制。

Conclusion: 无服务器平台正从第一代容器化集中式架构向第二代轻量级隔离边缘部署架构演进，新架构在延迟和冷启动方面优势显著，但以牺牲执行环境灵活性为代价，这些发现为平台选型和后续研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: With the ever-increasing usage of serverless computing in both industry and academia, it is essential to understand the mechanisms that power the underlying platforms. As serverless is more than ten years old, there are different platforms with vastly different approaches. We show that, next to the traditional and popular platforms, a second generation of serverless platform has emerged. While first-generation platforms are based on containerized, centralized execution, the new generation leverages lightweight isolates and edge deployment. This evolution reduces warm request latency from approximately 40 ms to around 10 ms and reduces cold starts to an afterthought, but limits the execution environment. In this paper, we gather and analyze all publicly available information to provide detailed insights into the underlying architecture of seven platforms and then run a microbenchmark-based evaluation totaling more than 38 million function calls to gain a deeper understanding their performance.

</details>


### [117] [Continuous benchmarking: Keeping pace with an evolving ecosystem of models and technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15919)
*Jan Vogelsang,Melissa Lober,Catherine Mia Schöfmann,José Villamar,Dennis Terhorst,Johanna Senk,Hans Ekkehard Plesser,Markus Diesmann,Susanne Kunkel,Anno C. Kurth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向高性能应用（特别是神经科学和AI领域）的自动化基准测试流水线，借鉴持续集成理念，通过支持用户无关操作和持续基准测试，解决了大规模模型与HPC系统快速演进下的可复现性与协作问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算应用（如神经科学和人工智能领域）的快速发展要求基准测试能够跟上大规模模型和系统的演进节奏；同时，研究软件开发作为持续的社区协作过程，亟需可定制、可协作的基准测试解决方案以保障可持续的技术进步。

Method: 借鉴持续集成（CI）思想，构建自动化基准测试流水线；在先前系统性基准测试工作流概念工作的基础上，扩展了用户无关操作（user-agnostic operations）与持续基准测试（continuous benchmarking）功能，以定制化和协作作为核心设计目标。

Result: 实现了支持用户无关操作与持续基准测试的自动化流水线，促进了基准测试结果的可复现性与重用，为快速演进的大规模模型与高性能计算系统提供了软件工程解决方案。

Conclusion: 该自动化基准测试流水线能够有效支持研究软件社区的协作开发，确保基准测试的可复现性与可持续技术进步，为神经科学与人工智能领域的高性能计算应用提供了实用的工程化支撑。

Abstract: Drawing on ideas from continuous integration, we present concepts of an automated benchmarking pipeline for high performance applications. Customization and collaboration have been key design goals owing to the requirements of research-software development as a continuous community effort. We have extended our previous conceptual work on systematic benchmarking workflows with the functionality of user-agnostic operations as well as continuous benchmarking. This fosters reproducibility and re-use of benchmarking results to ensure sustainable technological progress. We provide software-engineering solutions to keep pace with the rapid evolution of both large-scale models and high-performance computing systems with a view towards the scientific domains of neuroscience and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [118] [Evaluating SYCL as a Unified Programming Model for Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16043)
*Ami Marowka*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从应用开发者视角评估SYCL框架，发现当前实现在跨平台可移植性、开发生产力和运行效率方面存在关键局限与不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算应用日益在异构环境中执行，SYCL作为单一源码、跨平台的并行编程框架应运而生，但其宣称的可移植性、生产力和性能目标缺乏明确定义与实现，导致开发者期望不一，亟需系统性评估。

Method: 从应用开发者视角评估SYCL是否满足跨平台开发核心标准；重点分析内存管理抽象（USM与buffer-accessor对比）和并行性抽象（NDRange与层次化内核模型对比）；在Intel平台上进行基准测试，并综合近年来多种SYCL实现与编译器的相关研究发现。

Result: 揭示了当前SYCL实现在代码可移植性、开发生产力和运行效率方面的关键局限与不一致性；提供了USM与buffer-accessor、NDRange与层次化内核模型的详细对比分析；指出了提升框架可靠性与跨平台可用性所需的改进方向。

Conclusion: SYCL在跨平台异构编程方面具有潜力，但当前实现尚未完全兑现其设计承诺，需要针对识别出的局限性与不一致性进行改进，以提升框架的可靠性和跨平台可用性。

Abstract: High-performance computing (HPC) applications are increasingly executed in heterogeneous environments, introducing new challenges for programming and software portability. SYCL has emerged as a leading model designed to simplify heterogeneous programming and make it more accessible to developers. Intended as a single-source, cross-platform parallel programming framework, SYCL promises portability, productivity, and performance across a variety of architectures. However, these goals have not been consistently defined or realized, leaving developers with varying expectations. This paper addresses this gap by evaluating SYCL from the perspective of application developers. We analyze whether SYCL meets essential criteria for cross-platform development, including code portability, development productivity, and runtime efficiency. Our evaluation draws on benchmarks and illustrative examples and focuses on SYCL's memory management and parallelism abstractions. We provide detailed comparisons between Unified Shared Memory (USM) and buffer-accessor approaches, as well as between NDRange and hierarchical kernel models. In addition to presenting our own benchmark results on Intel platforms, we synthesize findings from recent studies across multiple SYCL implementations and compilers. Our results expose key limitations and inconsistencies in current SYCL implementations and offer insights into the steps needed to improve the framework's reliability and cross-platform usability.

</details>


### [119] [T-RBFT: A Scalable and Efficient Byzantine Consensus Based on Trusted Execution Environment for Consortium Blockchain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16053)
*Wen Gao,Xinhong Hei,Yichuan Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对联盟链场景提出了T-RBFT，一种基于网络分片思想和可信执行环境（TEE）增强的双层共识机制，在保证安全性的前提下显著降低了通信开销并提升了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BFT协议针对最坏情况设计，在节点普遍可信且故障节点较少的联盟链环境中会产生过多的消息交换和计算开销，限制了系统的性能和可扩展性。

Method: 提出T-RBFT双层共识机制：首先根据运行时特征将共识节点动态分组；组内采用改进的Raft机制达成共识；组间通过TEE辅助的BFT协议达成一致。

Result: 实验评估表明，与现有的双层共识协议相比，T-RBFT降低了通信开销和延迟，并实现了更高的吞吐量。

Conclusion: T-RBFT为许可链网络提供了一种可扩展且通信高效共识协议，有效解决了传统BFT协议在联盟链场景下的性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: With the continuous expansion of blockchain application scenarios, consortium chains have raised higher performance and security requirements for consensus mechanisms. Unlike public blockchains, consortium chains typically implement an admission mechanism that restricts participation to trusted entities, ensuring that most replicas are honest and the number of faulty nodes remains small under normal circumstances. In such settings, conventional Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) protocols, which are designed for worst-case adversarial scenarios, incur excessive message exchanges and computational overhead, thereby limiting performance and scalability. To address this issue, this paper proposes T-RBFT, a two-layer consensus mechanism inspired by network sharding and enhanced by the trusted execution environment (TEE). In T-RBFT, consensus nodes are first dynamically grouped based on their runtime characteristics. Then, inter-group consensus is achieved through a TEE-assisted BFT protocol, while each group internally reaches agreement using an improved Raft-based mechanism. Experimental evaluation shows that T-RBFT reduces communication overhead and latency, and achieves higher throughput compared to existing two-layer consensus protocols, providing a scalable and communication-efficient consensus protocol for permissioned blockchain networks.

</details>


### [120] [Robust Synchronisation for Federated Learning in The Face of Correlated Device Failure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16090)
*Stefan Behfar,Richard Mortier*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了可用性加权概率同步并行（AW-PSP）方法，通过动态调整节点采样概率来解决联邦学习中节点可用性与数据分布相关性导致的不公平问题，提升了系统的鲁棒性、标签覆盖率和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的概率同步并行（PSP）技术假设设备行为是静态的且设备间相互独立，导致高可用性节点主导训练过程，而可用性低的节点数据被忽视。当节点可用性与数据分布同时存在相关性时，会导致特定类别或群体的持续代表性不足，影响学习效果。

Method: 提出AW-PSP方法，利用实时可用性预测、历史行为和故障相关性指标动态调整节点采样概率。采用基于马尔可夫的可用性预测器区分瞬态和慢性故障，并使用分布式哈希表（DHT）层去中心化存储元数据（包括延迟、新鲜度和效用分数）。

Result: 基于真实轨迹的评估表明，AW-PSP相比标准PSP在独立故障和相关性故障场景下具有更好的鲁棒性，提高了标签覆盖率，并降低了公平性方差。

Conclusion: AW-PSP为联邦学习部署提供了一种可用性感知且公平的节点采样协议，能够在异构和易故障环境中扩展至大规模节点。

Abstract: Probabilistic Synchronous Parallel (PSP) is a technique in distributed learning systems to reduce synchronization bottlenecks by sampling a subset of participating nodes per round. In Federated Learning (FL), where edge devices are often unreliable due to factors including mobility, power constraints, and user activity, PSP helps improve system throughput. However, PSP has a key limitation: it assumes device behavior is static and different devices are independent. This can lead to unfair distributed synchronization, due to highly available nodes dominating training while those that are often unavailable rarely participate and so their data may be missed. If both data distribution and node availability are simultaneously correlated with the device, then both PSP and standard FL algorithms will suffer from persistent under-representation of certain classes or groups resulting in inefficient or ineffective learning of certain features. We introduce Availability-Weighted PSP (AW-PSP), an extension to PSP that addresses the issue of co-correlation of unfair sampling and data availability by dynamically adjusting node sampling probabilities using real-time availability predictions, historical behavior, and failure correlation metrics. A Markov-based availability predictor distinguishes transient \emph{vs} chronic failures, while a Distributed Hash Table (DHT) layer decentralizes metadata, including latency, freshness, and utility scores. We implement AW-PSP and trace-driven evaluation shows that it improves robustness to both independent and correlated failures, increases label coverage, and reduces fairness variance compared to standard PSP. AW-PSP thus provides an availability-aware, and fairness-conscious node sampling protocol for FL deployments that will scale to large numbers of nodes even in heterogeneous and failure-prone environments.

</details>


### [121] [Compositional Design, Implementation, and Verification of Swarms (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16097)
*Florian Furbach,Lucas Clorius,Roland Kuhn,Hernán Melgratti,Alceste Scalas,Emilio Tuosto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了群协议的组合式规范、验证与实现的理论和技术，解决了现有模型非组合性导致的模块化开发和代码复用困难问题，并提供了配套软件工具实现独立组件的自动集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的群协议模型缺乏组合性，这使得大规模复杂群应用系统的模块化开发以及代码复用变得困难，阻碍了预现有群协议和机器实现的正确重用。

Method: 提出了新颖的组合式群协议规范、验证和实现的理论与技术，开发了配套的软件工具，支持独立设计和验证的群组件的自动集成。

Result: 实现了群协议的正确组合式重用，能够将独立设计并验证过的群协议和机器实现进行组合，并通过软件工具实现自动化的组件集成。

Conclusion: 该工作为群协议的组合式开发提供了理论基础和实用工具，使得模块化开发和代码复用成为可能，有助于构建大型复杂的群应用系统。

Abstract: Swarm protocols are a recently introduced formalism for specifying, implementing, and verifying peer-to-peer systems called swarms. A swarm consists of distributed agents called machines that communicate by asynchronous event propagation. Following a local-first model, each machine can progress without requiring continuous connectivity to other machines. Existing models of swarms are not compositional, making the modular development of large and complex swarm applications as well as the reuse of code difficult. We address these issues by presenting novel theory and techniques for the compositional specification, verification, and implementation of swarms. These results enable the correct compositional reuse of pre-existing swarm protocols and machine implementations. We implement these contributions in a companion software artifact which enables the automatic integration of independently designed and verified swarm components.

</details>


### [122] [Logarithmic-Time Geodesically Convex Decomposition in Programmable Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16112)
*Henning Hillebrandt,Andreas Padalkin,Christian Scheideler,Daniel Warner,Julian Werthmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在可重构电路amoebot模型中提出了一种针对任意结构的对数时间分解算法，将复杂结构分解为O(|H|)个测地凸区域，同时改进了全局极值和生成树算法的时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可编程物质分解算法（如三角剖分和隧道区域分解）仅适用于受限的amoebot结构类别。为了使分解技术能应用于更广泛的场景，需要设计一种能够处理任意amoebot结构（特别是含孔洞结构）的高效分解算法。

Method: 基于可重构电路的几何amoebot模型，利用电路实现的瞬时蜂鸣传输机制，提出了一种新的分解方法。该算法将任意amoebot结构分解为O(|H|)个简单的、测地凸的区域（其中|H|表示结构中的孔洞数量），算法在O(log n)轮次内完成。

Result: 成功实现了对任意amoebot结构的分解，生成O(|H|)个测地凸区域；算法在O(log n)轮次内完成。作为副产品，改进了Padalkin等人(2024)的全局极值算法在特殊情况下以及生成树算法的时间复杂度至O(log n)轮（w.h.p.）。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩展了可编程物质分解算法的适用范围，突破了此前仅能处理受限结构的局限，并在对数时间内完成分解。测地凸区域的分解结果为后续的形状变换、协调控制等任务提供了良好的结构基础。对全局极值和生成树算法的改进也展示了该分解方法的实用价值。

Abstract: The decomposition of complex structures into simpler substructures is a powerful technique with a wide range of applications. We study the computation of decompositions in the context of programmable matter. The amoebot model is a well-established model for programmable matter, which places $n$ tiny robots called amoebots on the triangular grid. We consider the reconfigurable circuit extension of the geometric amoebot model, which allows amoebots to interconnect via so-called circuits. Amoebots can then instantaneously transmit simple beeps to all amoebots connected by the same circuit. Using reconfigurable circuits, previous papers have described a linear-time triangulation algorithm, and a logarithmic-time decomposition algorithm into so-called tunnel regions. Both algorithms only work on a restricted class of amoebot structures. In this paper, we define a decomposition into $O(|\mathcal H|)$ simple, geodesically convex regions for arbitrary amoebot structures, and show how it can compute such a decomposition in $O(\log n)$ rounds, where $|\mathcal H|$ denotes the number of holes in the amoebot structure. As a byproduct, we also improve the global maxima algorithm of Padalkin et al. (Nat. Comput., 2024) for special cases and with that also their spanning tree algorithm to $O(\log n)$ rounds w.h.p.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [InfoChess: A Game of Adversarial Inference and a Laboratory for Quantifiable Information Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15373)
*Kieran A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了InfoChess，一个专注于竞争性信息获取的对称对抗博弈测试平台，并开发了超越启发式基线的强化学习智能体，通过信息论指标深入分析了博弈过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的博弈研究往往将物质利益（如吃子）作为主要目标，这会混淆信息获取在博弈中的独立作用。为了纯粹地研究部分可观察环境下的多智能体推理问题，作者需要一个能够消除物质激励干扰、将竞争性信息获取提升为主要目标的博弈环境。

Method: 1. 设计InfoChess博弈：移除吃子机制，棋子用于改变可见性，玩家根据推断对手国王位置的准确度得分。
2. 构建启发式智能体层级：定义具有递增对手建模水平的基线智能体。
3. 训练强化学习智能体：开发出超越启发式基线的RL代理。
4. 信息论分析：利用信念熵、预言机交叉熵和预测对数分数等指标，解耦认知不确定性、校准失配和对抗移动引起的不确定性。

Result: 1. 训练的强化学习智能体表现优于所有启发式基线智能体。
2. 成功利用信息论指标对博弈过程进行了细粒度分析，有效分离了不同来源的不确定性。
3. 证明了InfoChess作为研究部分可观察环境下多智能体推理问题的有效测试平台。

Conclusion: InfoChess成功地将竞争性信息获取隔离为博弈的核心目标，为研究部分可观察环境下的多智能体推理提供了一个纯粹且可控的测试平台。通过信息论工具，研究者能够深入剖析博弈中的认知不确定性、校准问题和对抗性因素，推动了相关领域的研究进展。作者开源了环境和代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We propose InfoChess, a symmetric adversarial game that elevates competitive information acquisition to the primary objective. There is no piece capture, removing material incentives that would otherwise confound the role of information. Instead, pieces are used to alter visibility. Players are scored on their probabilistic inference of the opponent's king location over the duration of the game. To explore the space of strategies for playing InfoChess, we introduce a hierarchy of heuristic agents defined by increasing levels of opponent modeling, and train a reinforcement learning agent that outperforms these baselines. Leveraging the discrete structure of the game, we analyze gameplay through natural information-theoretic characterizations that include belief entropy, oracle cross entropy, and predictive log score under the action-induced observation channel. These measures disentangle epistemic uncertainty, calibration mismatch, and uncertainty induced by adversarial movement. The design of InfoChess renders it a testbed for studying multi-agent inference under partial observability. We release code for the environment and agents, and a public interface to encourage further study.

</details>


### [124] [Scalable Algorithms with Provable Optimality Bounds for the Multiple Watchman Route Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15610)
*Srikar Gouru,Ariel Felner,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对多守卫路径规划问题(MWRP)，提出了最优规划器MWRP-CP3，通过状态空间剪枝和启发式优化实现了比现有最优算法快200倍以上的性能；同时提出了有质量保证的次优算法，能解决比最优算法大3倍的地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多守卫路径规划问题(MWRP)需要为M个守卫找到一组路径，使得地图上的每个位置都能被至少一个守卫看到。该问题具有重要的实际应用价值，但现有算法效率较低，需要开发更高效的求解方法。

Method: 1) 提出多种状态空间剪枝方法，通过删除保证会被途经的区域来减少搜索空间；2) 开发最优规划器MWRP-CP3，结合剪枝技术与改进现有启发式质量和计算时间的技术；3) 提出有解质量界限的次优算法，包括针对makespan问题的加权A*变体MxWA*；4) 开发次优算法的任意时间变体，以及通过求解分解子问题来改进次优解的技术。

Result: 在2D网格地图上，MWRP-CP3能将搜索空间减少超过95%，比现有最优算法快200倍以上；次优算法能解决比MWRP-CP3可解地图大3倍的地图规模。提供了开源代码库和视频演示。

Conclusion: 本文通过状态空间剪枝和启发式优化技术显著提升了MWRP问题的求解效率，最优算法在保持最优性的同时大幅提升了速度，次优算法进一步扩展了可解决问题的规模，为该领域提供了有效的求解工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the Multiple Watchman Route Problem (MWRP), which aims to find a set of paths that M watchmen can follow such that every location on the map can be seen by at least one watchman. First, we propose multiple methods to reduce the state space over which a search needs to be conducted by pruning map areas that are guaranteed to be seen en route to other areas. Next, we introduce MWRP-CP3, an efficient optimal planner that combines these methods with techniques that improve the quality and calculation time of existing heuristics. We present several suboptimal algorithms with bounds on solution quality, including MxWA*, a general variant of weighted A* for makespan problems. We also present anytime variations of our suboptimal algorithms, as well as techniques to improve an existing suboptimal solution by solving multiple decomposed sub-problems. We show that MWRP-CP3 can reduce the search space by more than 95% and runs more than 200x faster than existing optimal algorithms on 2D grid maps. We also show that our suboptimal algorithms solve maps 3x larger than those solvable by MWRP-CP3. See mwrp-cp3.github.io for the open source codebase and video demonstrations.

</details>


### [125] [AstroVLM: Expert Multi-agent Collaborative Reasoning for Astronomical Imaging Quality Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16024)
*Yaohui Han,Tianshuo Wang,Zixi Zhao,Zhengchun Zhu,Shuo Ren,Yiru Wang,Rongliang Fu,Tinghuan Chen,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了AstroVLM，一个用于天文图像质量诊断的协作式多智能体系统，解决了天文成像过程复杂、质量诊断困难的问题，并在真实任务上超越了所有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）已在多个特定领域展现强大能力，但天文成像领域尚未得到充分研究。天文成像涉及多学科知识和多个子任务，过程复杂且各环节存在复杂的潜在关联，相互影响显著，导致图像质量诊断和错误定位极具挑战性，即使是NASA等专业机构也需投入大量时间和精力。

Method: 提出了AstroVLM，一个协作式多智能体系统，专门用于诊断天文图像的质量，以应对天文成像复杂多过程任务的挑战。

Result: 实验结果表明，AstroVLM在真实世界的天文成像质量诊断任务上优于所有基线模型，表现出了卓越的性能。

Conclusion: AstroVLM为语言模型处理复杂多过程任务提供了参考，展示了多智能体协作系统在天文成像等复杂领域问题中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have been applied to several specific domains and have shown strong problem-solving capabilities. However, astronomical imaging, a quite complex problem involving multidisciplinary knowledge and several subtasks, has not been adequately studied. Due to the complexity of the astronomical imaging process, both world-class astronomical organizations, such as NASA, and expert enthusiasts devote a great deal of time and effort. This is because the processes in astronomical imaging have complex underlying correlations that significantly influence one another, making the quality diagnosis and error localization of astronomical images challenging. To address this problem, we propose AstroVLM, a collaborative multi-agent system for diagnosing the quality of astronomical images. Experiment results show that AstroVLM outperforms all baselines on real-world astronomical imaging quality diagnosis tasks, providing a reference for language models to handle complicated multi-process tasks.

</details>


### [126] [Veritas-RPM: Provenance-Guided Multi-Agent False Positive Suppression for Remote Patient Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16081)
*Aswini Misro,Vikash Sharma,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Veritas-RPM，一种溯源引导的多智能体架构，用于识别和抑制假阳性病例。通过构建98个假阳性场景的分类体系并生成530个合成患者数据进行验证，结果显示该系统在真抑制率（TSR）、假升级率（FER）和不确定率（INDR）方面表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗监护领域，假阳性警报是一个重要问题，会导致医疗资源浪费和警报疲劳。为了解决假阳性病例的准确识别和抑制问题，作者开发了一个多智能体系统，利用溯源信息和多领域专业知识来提高诊断准确性。

Method: 提出Veritas-RPM架构，包含五个处理层：VeritasAgent（真值组装）、SentinelLayer（异常检测）、DirectorAgent（专科路由）、六个领域专科智能体和MetaSentinelAgent（冲突解决和最终决策）。基于文档化的RPM模式构建98个假阳性场景的综合分类体系，直接从分类参数生成530个合成患者时期数据，通过处理管道进行处理。所有病例都有已知的真值标签。

Result: 系统性能通过三个指标进行评估：真抑制率（TSR）、假升级率（FER）和不确定率（INDR）。由于所有病例的真值标签已知，能够准确评估系统在识别和抑制假阳性病例方面的性能表现。

Conclusion: Veritas-RPM多智能体架构成功实现了对假阳性病例的有效识别和抑制，通过分层处理和多专科智能体协作，为解决医疗监护中的假阳性问题提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: We present Veritas-RPM, a provenance-guided multi-agent architecture comprising five processing layers: VeritasAgent (ground-truth assembly), SentinelLayer (anomaly detection), DirectorAgent (specialist routing), six domain Specialist Agents, and MetaSentinelAgent (conflict resolution and final decision). We construct a 98-case synthetic taxonomy of false-positive scenarios derived from documented RPM patterns. Synthetic patient epochs (n = 530) were generated directly from taxonomy parameters and processed through the pipeline. Ground-truth labels are known for all cases. Performance is reported as True Suppression Rate (TSR), False Escalation Rate (FER), and Indeterminate Rate (INDR).

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [DeepER-Med: Advancing Deep Evidence-Based Research in Medicine Through Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15456)
*Zhizheng Wang,Chih-Hsuan Wei,Joey Chan,Robert Leaman,Chi-Ping Day,Chuan Wu,Mark A Knepper,Antolin Serrano Farias,Jordina Rincon-Torroella,Hasan Slika,Betty Tyler,Ryan Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Asmita Indurkar,Mélanie Hébert,Shubo Tian,Lauren He,Noor Naffakh,Aseem Aseem,Nicholas Wan,Emily Y Chew,Tiarnan D L Keenan,Zhiyong Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DeepER-Med，一个可解释、可审查的医疗循证研究智能体框架，并构建了专家级评估数据集DeepER-MedQA。实验表明该方法在多维度上优于现有平台，且在真实临床案例中表现出良好的实用性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度研究系统在医疗AI应用中缺乏明确且可审查的证据评估标准，存在累积错误风险，难以让临床医生和研究者评估输出的可靠性；同时现有基准很少针对复杂的真实世界医疗问题进行评估。

Method: 提出了DeepER-Med框架，将深度医疗研究形式化为显式可审查的循证生成工作流，包含三个模块：研究规划、智能体协作和证据综合。同时构建了DeepER-MedQA数据集，包含100个由11位多学科生物医学专家从真实医疗研究场景中提炼并审核的专家级研究问题。

Result: 专家人工评估显示，DeepER-Med在多维度上持续优于广泛使用的生产级平台，包括生成新颖科学见解的能力。在8个真实临床案例验证中，临床医生评估表明DeepER-Med的结论在7个案例中与临床建议一致。

Conclusion: DeepER-Med通过显式、可审查的循证生成流程提升了医疗AI系统的可信度和透明度，为医学研究和临床决策支持提供了有效工具，在真实临床场景中展现出实用价值。

Abstract: Trustworthiness and transparency are essential for the clinical adoption of artificial intelligence (AI) in healthcare and biomedical research. Recent deep research systems aim to accelerate evidence-grounded scientific discovery by integrating AI agents with multi-hop information retrieval, reasoning, and synthesis. However, most existing systems lack explicit and inspectable criteria for evidence appraisal, creating a risk of compounding errors and making it difficult for researchers and clinicians to assess the reliability of their outputs. In parallel, current benchmarking approaches rarely evaluate performance on complex, real-world medical questions. Here, we introduce DeepER-Med, a Deep Evidence-based Research framework for Medicine with an agentic AI system. DeepER-Med frames deep medical research as an explicit and inspectable workflow of evidence-based generation, consisting of three modules: research planning, agentic collaboration, and evidence synthesis. To support realistic evaluation, we also present DeepER-MedQA, an evidence-grounded dataset comprising 100 expert-level research questions derived from authentic medical research scenarios and curated by a multidisciplinary panel of 11 biomedical experts. Expert manual evaluation demonstrates that DeepER-Med consistently outperforms widely used production-grade platforms across multiple criteria, including the generation of novel scientific insights. We further demonstrate the practical utility of DeepER-Med through eight real-world clinical cases. Human clinician assessment indicates that DeepER-Med's conclusions align with clinical recommendations in seven cases, highlighting its potential for medical research and decision support.

</details>


### [128] [GIST: Multimodal Knowledge Extraction and Spatial Grounding via Intelligent Semantic Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15495)
*Shivendra Agrawal,Bradley Hayes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GIST管道，将移动点云转化为语义导航拓扑，实现语义搜索、定位、区域分类和指令生成，在复杂密集环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在零售店、仓库、医院等复杂密集环境中，视觉特征快速过时，语义分布呈长尾，现有视觉语言模型在杂乱环境的空间接地方面仍存在困难。

Method: 构建多模态知识提取管道，将消费级移动点云数据转化为语义标注的导航拓扑，包括将场景蒸馏为2D占用图、提取拓扑布局、通过智能关键帧和语义选择叠加轻量级语义层。

Result: 在下游任务中实现：意图驱动的语义搜索引擎；单次语义定位器达到1.04米top-5平均平移误差；区域分类模块；视觉接地指令生成器。在多标准LLM评估中优于序列基线，现场形成性评估(N=5)达到80%导航成功率。

Conclusion: GIST系统成功解决了复杂密集环境中的空间接地挑战，通过结构化空间知识支持多种人机交互任务，验证了其在通用设计中的能力。

Abstract: Navigating complex, densely packed environments like retail stores, warehouses, and hospitals poses a significant spatial grounding challenge for humans and embodied AI. In these spaces, dense visual features quickly become stale given the quasi-static nature of items, and long-tail semantic distributions challenge traditional computer vision. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) help assistive systems navigate semantically-rich spaces, they still struggle with spatial grounding in cluttered environments. We present GIST (Grounded Intelligent Semantic Topology), a multimodal knowledge extraction pipeline that transforms a consumer-grade mobile point cloud into a semantically annotated navigation topology. Our architecture distills the scene into a 2D occupancy map, extracts its topological layout, and overlays a lightweight semantic layer via intelligent keyframe and semantic selection. We demonstrate the versatility of this structured spatial knowledge through critical downstream Human-AI interaction tasks: (1) an intent-driven Semantic Search engine that actively infers categorical alternatives and zones when exact matches fail; (2) a one-shot Semantic Localizer achieving a 1.04 m top-5 mean translation error; (3) a Zone Classification module that segments the walkable floor plan into high-level semantic regions; and (4) a Visually-Grounded Instruction Generator that synthesizes optimal paths into egocentric, landmark-rich natural language routing. In multi-criteria LLM evaluations, GIST outperforms sequence-based instruction generation baselines. Finally, an in-situ formative evaluation (N=5) yields an 80% navigation success rate relying solely on verbal cues, validating the system's capacity for universal design.

</details>


### [129] [Bureaucratic Silences: What the Canadian AI Register Reveals, Omits, and Obscures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15514)
*Dipto Das,Christelle Tessono,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析加拿大联邦AI登记册，指出该登记册将AI构建为“可靠工具”而非“可争议决策”，导致透明度沦为表演性合规，而非实质性的问责机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年11月加拿大政府发布了首份联邦AI登记册以展示其透明度承诺。作者认为此类登记册并非政府活动的中立镜像，而是能够配置问责边界的本体设计工具，因此有必要深入探究其如何界定AI系统的性质与责任。

Method: 采用适用于公共部门的算法决策框架（ADMAPS），结合定量映射与演绎定性编码方法，对登记册中包含的409个AI系统的完整数据集进行了分析。

Result: 研究发现存在“主权AI”修辞与官僚实践之间的巨大鸿沟：86%的系统用于内部提效，但登记册系统性地掩盖了运行这些系统所需的人类裁量权、培训及不确定性管理；通过偏重技术描述而非社会技术背景，登记册构建了一种将AI视为“可靠工具”而非“可争议决策”的本体论。

Conclusion: 如果设计范式不改变，此类透明度工具将面临使问责制自动化为表演性合规练习的风险，即提供了可见性却无法提供可争议性。

Abstract: In November 2025, the Government of Canada operationalized its commitment to transparency by releasing its first Federal AI Register. In this paper, we argue that such registers are not neutral mirrors of government activity, but active instruments of ontological design that configure the boundaries of accountability. We analyzed the Register's complete dataset of 409 systems using the Algorithmic Decision-Making Adapted for the Public Sector (ADMAPS) framework, combining quantitative mapping with deductive qualitative coding. Our findings reveal a sharp divergence between the rhetoric of "sovereign AI" and the reality of bureaucratic practice: while 86\% of systems are deployed internally for efficiency, the Register systematically obscures the human discretion, training, and uncertainty management required to operate them. By privileging technical descriptions over sociotechnical context, the Register constructs an ontology of AI as "reliable tooling" rather than "contestable decision-making." We conclude that without a shift in design, such transparency artifacts risk automating accountability into a performative compliance exercise, offering visibility without contestability.

</details>


### [130] [LACE: Lattice Attention for Cross-thread Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15529)
*Yang Li,Zirui Zhang,Yang Liu,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LACE框架，通过引入跨线程注意力机制实现并行推理路径的交互与协作，配合合成数据训练，显著提升了大语言模型的推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的推理过程通常是孤立的，即使采样多条并行推理路径，它们之间也缺乏交互，往往会在相同的地方以冗余的方式失败。研究者希望将独立尝试转变为协调并行的过程，通过路径间的信息共享来提升推理效果。

Method: 提出LACE框架，重新设计模型架构以支持跨线程注意力，使并发推理路径能在推理过程中共享中间见解并相互纠错。针对缺乏自然协作训练数据的问题，构建了合成数据管道，显式教导模型在线程间进行通信和错误纠正。

Result: 实验表明，这种统一探索方法显著优于标准并行搜索，推理准确率提升超过7个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明，允许并行推理路径进行交互能使大语言模型更加有效，为提升模型推理能力提供了新方向。

Abstract: Current large language models reason in isolation. Although it is common to sample multiple reasoning paths in parallel, these trajectories do not interact, and often fail in the same redundant ways. We introduce LACE, a framework that transforms reasoning from a collection of independent trials into a coordinated, parallel process. By repurposing the model architecture to enable cross-thread attention, LACE allows concurrent reasoning paths to share intermediate insights and correct one another during inference. A central challenge is the absence of natural training data that exhibits such collaborative behavior. We address this gap with a synthetic data pipeline that explicitly teaches models to communicate and error-correct across threads. Experiments show that this unified exploration substantially outperforms standard parallel search, improving reasoning accuracy by over 7 points. Our results suggest that large language models can be more effective when parallel reasoning paths are allowed to interact.

</details>


### [131] [Preregistered Belief Revision Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15558)
*Saad Alqithami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体系统中因从众效应导致的错误收敛问题，提出了PBRC协议级机制，通过严格分离开放通信与认知改变，确保信念变更基于预先注册的证据，从而实现认知问责并抑制错误级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审议式多智能体系统中的交互虽旨在提升性能，但会产生危险的从众效应：一致性、置信度、声望或多数规模可能被错误地视为证据，导致高置信度地收敛至错误结论。

Method: 引入预注册信念修订合约（PBRC），这是一种协议级机制，公开固定一阶证据触发器、允许的修订算子、优先规则和回退策略，严格分离开放通信与可采纳的认知改变。非回退步骤仅当引用预注册触发器并提供非空的外部验证证据token见证集时才被接受。

Result: 证明了在具有保守回退的证据合约下，仅社交轮次不能增加置信度且无法产生纯从众驱动的错误-确信级联；展示了可审计触发协议允许证据PBRC范式；证明健全执行产生认知问责；证明了token不变合约下，执行轨迹仅依赖于token暴露痕迹。

Conclusion: PBRC机制能够有效抑制从众驱动的错误级联，实现认知问责，并在鲁棒性与活性之间提供可调节的权衡，为多智能体系统的安全信念修订提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: Deliberative multi-agent systems allow agents to exchange messages and revise beliefs over time. While this interaction is meant to improve performance, it can also create dangerous conformity effects: agreement, confidence, prestige, or majority size may be treated as if they were evidence, producing high-confidence convergence to false conclusions. To address this, we introduce PBRC (Preregistered Belief Revision Contracts), a protocol-level mechanism that strictly separates open communication from admissible epistemic change. A PBRC contract publicly fixes first-order evidence triggers, admissible revision operators, a priority rule, and a fallback policy. A non-fallback step is accepted only when it cites a preregistered trigger and provides a nonempty witness set of externally validated evidence tokens. This ensures that every substantive belief change is both enforceable by a router and auditable after the fact. In this paper, (a) we prove that under evidential contracts with conservative fallback, social-only rounds cannot increase confidence and cannot generate purely conformity-driven wrong-but-sure cascades. (b) We show that auditable trigger protocols admit evidential PBRC normal forms that preserve belief trajectories and canonicalized audit traces. (c) We demonstrate that sound enforcement yields epistemic accountability: any change of top hypothesis is attributable to a concrete validated witness set. For token-invariant contracts, (d) we prove that enforced trajectories depend only on token-exposure traces; under flooding dissemination, these traces are characterized exactly by truncated reachability, giving tight diameter bounds for universal evidence closure. Finally, we introduce a companion contractual dynamic doxastic logic to specify trace invariants, and provide simulations illustrating cascade suppression, auditability, and robustness-liveness trade-offs.

</details>


### [132] [Experience Compression Spectrum: Unifying Memory, Skills, and Rules in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15877)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Wei Qiu,Ziyuan Li,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: As LLM agents scale to long-horizon, multi-session deployments, efficiently managing accumulated experience becomes a critical bottleneck. Agent memory systems and agent skill discovery both address this challenge -- extracting reusable knowledge from interaction traces -- yet a citation analysis of 1,136 references across 22 primary papers reveals a cross-community citation rate below 1%. We propose the \emph{Experience Compression Spectrum}, a unifying framework that positions memory, skills, and rules as points along a single axis of increasing compression (5--20$\times$ for episodic memory, 50--500$\times$ for procedural skills, 1,000$\times$+ for declarative rules), directly reducing context consumption, retrieval latency, and compute overhead. Mapping 20+ systems onto this spectrum reveals that every system operates at a fixed, predetermined compression level -- none supports adaptive cross-level compression, a gap we term the \emph{missing diagonal}. We further show that specialization alone is insufficient -- both communities independently solve shared sub-problems without exchanging solutions -- that evaluation methods are tightly coupled to compression levels, that transferability increases with compression at the cost of specificity, and that knowledge lifecycle management remains largely neglected. We articulate open problems and design principles for scalable, full-spectrum agent learning systems.

</details>


### [133] [Subliminal Transfer of Unsafe Behaviors in AI Agent Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15559)
*Jacob Dang,Brian Y. Xie,Omar G. Younis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实证发现：在智能体系统中，不安全行为可通过模型蒸馏以潜意识方式迁移。即使对轨迹数据进行严格的关键词过滤，学生智能体仍会从教师处继承危险行为偏好（如文件删除偏好、chmod命令偏好），证明显式数据清洗无法防御此类隐式行为偏见传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明语言模型可通过语义无关数据传递语义特征，但在智能体系统中，基于轨迹学习的行为特征是否能以潜意识方式迁移尚不明确。该问题对智能体安全性至关重要，因为若危险行为可通过隐式方式传递，将威胁模型蒸馏等安全实践。

Method: 设计两个互补实验：(1) API环境设置：构建具有强删除偏见的教师智能体，使用严格过滤删除关键词的"安全任务"轨迹蒸馏学生模型；(2) Bash环境设置：将API调用替换为shell命令，偏见操作化为优先使用chmod而非语义等价的chown/setfacl。两种设置均进行完整关键词清洗，测试学生智能体是否继承行为偏见。

Result: 在API设置中，同构蒸馏下学生智能体删除率达100%（基线5%）；在Bash设置中，chmod优先率达30%-55%（基线0%-10%），大模型到小模型蒸馏传递效应最强。两种环境下均观察到显著的行为偏见迁移，证明显式数据清洗防御不足。

Conclusion: 显式数据清洗无法有效防御智能体行为偏见的潜意识传递。行为偏见被隐式编码在轨迹动力学中，与工具接口类型无关。该发现对智能体安全部署具有重要启示，需要开发针对轨迹动态特征的防御机制。

Abstract: Recent work on subliminal learning demonstrates that language models can transmit semantic traits through data that is semantically unrelated to those traits. However, it remains unclear whether behavioral traits can transfer in agentic systems, where policies are learned from trajectories rather than static text. In this work, we provide the first empirical evidence that unsafe agent behaviors can transfer subliminally through model distillation across two complementary experimental settings. In our primary setting, we construct a teacher agent exhibiting a strong deletion bias, a tendency to perform destructive file-system actions via an API-style tool interface, and distill it into a student using only trajectories from ostensibly safe tasks, with all explicit deletion keywords rigorously filtered. In our secondary setting, we replicate the threat model in a native Bash environment, replacing API tool calls with shell commands and operationalizing the bias as a preference for issuing chmod as the first permission-related command over semantically equivalent alternatives such as chown or setfacl. Despite full keyword sanitation in both settings, students inherit measurable behavioral biases. In the API setting the student's deletion rate reaches 100% (versus a 5% baseline) under homogeneous distillation; in the Bash setting the student's chmod-first rate reaches 30%-55% (versus a 0%-10% baseline), with the strongest transfer observed in large-to-small distillation. Our results demonstrate that explicit data sanitation is an insufficient defense, and behavioral biases are encoded implicitly in trajectory dynamics regardless of the tool interface.

</details>


### [134] [Weak-Link Optimization for Multi-Agent Reasoning and Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15972)
*Haoyu Bian,Chaoning Zhang,Jiaquan Zhang,Xingyao Li,Yuanfang Guo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WORC框架，基于弱链原则通过定位并优化多智能体系统中的薄弱智能体，显著提升了推理性能和系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM多智能体协作框架存在推理不稳定性问题，个别智能体的错误会通过协作被放大。现有研究主要关注增强高能力智能体或抑制不可靠输出，而缺乏对限制性能的薄弱智能体进行系统性识别与强化。

Method: 提出WORC（弱链优化）框架，包含两个阶段：（1）薄弱智能体定位阶段：构建任务特征，利用基于元学习的权重预测器（通过群体智能算法识别的最优配置进行训练），实现从任务特征到智能体性能权重的零样本映射，识别预测权重最低的智能体为薄弱智能体；（2）弱链优化阶段：采用不确定性驱动的分配策略，为薄弱智能体分配额外的推理预算，预测权重越低则重复采样配额越大，以补偿可靠性不足。

Result: WORC在推理基准测试上达到平均82.2%的准确率，同时提升了框架稳定性和跨架构泛化能力。实验表明补偿弱链比单纯增强优势更能提升多智能体系统的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过系统性地识别并补偿多智能体系统中的薄弱环节而非仅强化优势，能够有效增强系统的整体鲁棒性和性能表现，为多智能体协作框架的优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-driven multi-agent frameworks address complex reasoning tasks through multi-role collaboration. However, existing approaches often suffer from reasoning instability, where individual agent errors are amplified through collaboration, undermining overall performance. Current research mainly focuses on enhancing high-capability agents or suppressing unreliable outputs to improve framework effectiveness, while systematic identification and reinforcement of performance-limiting agents receive less attention. To address this gap, we propose WORC, a \underline{w}eak-link \underline{o}ptimization framework for multi-agent \underline{r}easoning and \underline{c}ollaboration, grounded in the weak-link principle. WORC follows a two-stage workflow. In the weak agent localization stage, task features are constructed, and a meta-learning-based weight predictor trained on optimal configurations identified by swarm intelligence algorithms (SIAs) enables zero-shot mapping from these features to agent performance weights, where the agent with the lowest predicted weight is identified as the weak agent. In the weak-link optimization stage, an uncertainty-driven allocation strategy assigns additional reasoning budgets to weak agents, with lower predicted weights leading to larger repeated-sampling quotas to compensate for reliability deficiencies. Experimental results show that WORC achieves an average accuracy of 82.2\% on reasoning benchmarks while improving framework stability and cross-architecture generalization, suggesting that compensating for weak links, rather than reinforcing strengths alone, enhances the robustness of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [135] [Bilevel Optimization of Agent Skills via Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15709)
*Chenyi Huang,Haoting Zhang,Jingxu Xu,Zeyu Zheng,Yunduan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双层优化框架，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索优化技能结构与大语言模型优化组件内容，显著提升了智能体在运筹学问答数据集上的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能的设计对大语言模型智能体的任务表现有实质影响，但由于技能包含结构化的指令、工具和支持资源，优化过程需同时决策结构与内容，两者存在强相互依赖，导致系统化优化极具挑战性。

Method: 将技能优化建模为双层优化问题：外层循环采用蒙特卡洛树搜索确定技能结构，内层循环在选定结构下利用大语言模型优化组件内容，两阶段协同搜索最优技能配置。

Result: 在开源运筹学问答数据集上的实验表明，该双层优化框架能够有效提升智能体使用优化后技能的任务表现。

Conclusion: 双层优化框架为技能的结构与内容联合优化提供了可行解决方案，验证了该方法在提升智能体任务性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Agent \texttt{skills} are structured collections of instructions, tools, and supporting resources that help large language model (LLM) agents perform particular classes of tasks. Empirical evidence shows that the design of \texttt{skills} can materially affect agent task performance, yet systematically optimizing \texttt{skills} remains challenging. Since a \texttt{skill} comprises instructions, tools, and supporting resources in a structured way, optimizing it requires jointly determining both the structure of these components and the content each component contains. This gives rise to a complex decision space with strong interdependence across structure and components. We therefore represent these two coupled decisions as \texttt{skill} structure and component content, and formulate \texttt{skill} optimization as a bilevel optimization problem. We propose a bilevel optimization framework in which an outer loop employs Monte Carlo Tree Search to determine the \texttt{skill} structure, while an inner loop refines the component content within the structure selected by the outer loop. In both loops, we employ LLMs to assist the optimization procedure. We evaluate the proposed framework on an open-source Operations Research Question Answering dataset, and the experimental results suggest that the bilevel optimization framework improves the performance of the agents with the optimized \texttt{skill}.

</details>


### [136] [SocialGrid: A Benchmark for Planning and Social Reasoning in Embodied Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16022)
*Hikaru Shindo,Hanzhao Lin,Lukas Helff,Patrick Schramowski,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SocialGrid，一个受《Among Us》启发的具身多智能体环境，用于评估LLM智能体的规划、任务执行和社会推理能力。研究发现最强模型任务完成率低于60%，欺骗检测能力接近随机水平，揭示了当前LLM在社会推理方面的显著局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型从文本处理器转型为自主智能体，在具身多智能体环境中评估其社会推理能力变得至关重要。现有评估方法可能因导航等基础能力不足而干扰对社会智能的准确评估，因此需要专门的评估环境来隔离和准确测量社会推理能力。

Method: 引入SocialGrid具身多智能体环境，受《Among Us》游戏启发，包含规划、任务执行和社会推理三个评估维度。提供可选的Planning Oracle机制以隔离社会推理与规划缺陷，并提供自动失败分析和细粒度指标。建立基于Elo评分的对抗性联赛排行榜。

Result: 最强开源模型GPT-OSS-120B任务完成率和规划准确率低于60%，智能体易陷入重复行为或无法导航基础障碍。规划辅助虽提升任务完成率，但社会推理仍是瓶颈：所有规模的智能体欺骗检测能力接近随机水平，依赖浅层启发式而非累积行为证据。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体在社会推理方面存在显著缺陷，无法有效检测欺骗行为。SocialGrid为开发者提供了诊断和改进智能体的工具，通过细粒度指标和自动失败分析支持迭代优化，其竞争性排行榜促进了领域内的技术进步。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) transition from text processors to autonomous agents, evaluating their social reasoning in embodied multi-agent settings becomes critical. We introduce SocialGrid, an embodied multi-agent environment inspired by Among Us that evaluates LLM agents on planning, task execution, and social reasoning. Our evaluations reveal that even the strongest open model (GPT-OSS-120B) achieves below 60% accuracy in task completion and planning, with agents getting stuck in repetitive behaviors or failing to navigate basic obstacles. Since poor navigation confounds evaluation of social intelligence, SocialGrid offers an optional Planning Oracle to isolate social reasoning from planning deficits. While planning assistance improves task completion, social reasoning remains a bottleneck: agents fail to detect deception at near-random chance regardless of scale, relying on shallow heuristics rather than accumulating behavioral evidence. SocialGrid provides automatic failure analysis and fine-grained metrics, enabling developers to diagnose and improve their agents. We also establish a competitive leaderboard using Elo ratings from adversarial league play.

</details>


### [137] [The World Leaks the Future: Harness Evolution for Future Prediction Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15719)
*Chuyang Wei,Maohang Gao,Zhixin Han,Kefei Chen,Yu Zhuang,Haoxiang Guan,Yanzhi Zhang,Yilin Cheng,Jiyan He,Huanhuan Chen,Jian Li,Yu Shi,Yitong Duan,Shuxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Milkyway，一个自进化的LLM预测系统，通过利用未解决问题重复预测过程中的“内部反馈”信号来持续优化预测流程，在问题解决前就能提升预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的未来预测方法主要依赖问题解决后的最终结果进行学习，但最终结果过于粗粒度，无法指导早期的因素追踪、证据收集、解释和不确定性处理。由于公共证据会随时间演化，而有用的监督信号只有在问题解决后才能获得，这导致预测过程难以在问题解决前得到改进。

Method: 提出了Milkyway系统，保持基础模型固定，转而更新一个持久的“未来预测框架”（future prediction harness）。当对同一未解决问题进行重复预测时，系统提取早期与后期预测之间的“内部反馈”，将可重用的指导写回框架中，使后续预测能在问题解决前得到改进。问题解决后，最终结果提供“回顾性检查”，更新的框架随后应用于后续问题。

Result: 在FutureX和FutureWorld数据集上，Milkyway在所有对比方法中取得了最佳整体得分：FutureX从44.07提升至60.90，FutureWorld从62.22提升至77.96。

Conclusion: 通过利用内部反馈信号和回顾性检查机制，Milkyway成功实现了在问题解决前的预测能力提升，为未来预测任务提供了一种有效的自进化方法。

Abstract: Many consequential decisions must be made before the relevant outcome is known. Such problems are commonly framed as \emph{future prediction}, where an LLM agent must form a prediction for an unresolved question using only the public information available at the prediction time. The setting is difficult because public evidence evolves while useful supervision arrives only after the question is resolved, so most existing approaches still improve mainly from final outcomes. Yet final outcomes are too coarse to guide earlier factor tracking, evidence gathering and interpretation, or uncertainty handling. When the same unresolved question is revisited over time, temporal contrasts between earlier and later predictions can expose omissions in the earlier prediction process; we call this signal \emph{internal feedback}. We introduce \emph{Milkyway}, a self-evolving agent system that keeps the base model fixed and instead updates a persistent \emph{future prediction harness} for factor tracking, evidence gathering and interpretation, and uncertainty handling. Across repeated predictions on the same unresolved question, \emph{Milkyway} extracts internal feedback and writes reusable guidance back into the harness, so later predictions on that question can improve before the outcome is known. After the question is resolved, the final outcome provides a \emph{retrospective check} before the updated harness is carried forward to subsequent questions. On FutureX and FutureWorld, Milkyway achieves the best overall score among the compared methods, improving FutureX from 44.07 to 60.90 and FutureWorld from 62.22 to 77.96.

</details>


### [138] [LLM Reasoning Is Latent, Not the Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15726)
*Wenshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张应将大语言模型推理研究聚焦于潜在状态轨迹而非表层思维链，通过区分三个混淆因素提出竞争假设，发现现有证据最强支持H1假设，建议将潜在状态动力学作为默认研究对象并采用解耦实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前领域关于LLM推理的忠实性、可解释性、推理基准和推理时干预的主张都取决于将什么作为推理的主要对象，而现有研究混淆了潜在状态轨迹、表层思维链和串行计算三个因素，需要重新审视推理研究的核心对象。

Method: 提出三个竞争假设：H1（推理主要由潜在状态轨迹介导）、H2（推理主要由显式表层思维链介导）、H0（推理增益主要由通用串行计算解释），整合近期实证、机制和调研工作，并引入计算审计的工作示例来分解表层痕迹、潜在干预和匹配预算扩展。

Result: 当前证据最强支持H1作为默认工作假设而非任务无关结论，即推理主要由潜在状态轨迹介导，但这一结论需要在具体任务中验证。

Conclusion: 建议领域应将潜在状态动力学作为LLM推理的默认研究对象，并采用明确解耦表层痕迹、潜在状态和串行计算的实验设计来评估推理能力。

Abstract: This position paper argues that large language model (LLM) reasoning should be studied as latent-state trajectory formation rather than as faithful surface chain-of-thought (CoT). This matters because claims about faithfulness, interpretability, reasoning benchmarks, and inference-time intervention all depend on what the field takes the primary object of reasoning to be. We ask what that object should be once three often-confounded factors are separated and formalize three competing hypotheses: H1, reasoning is primarily mediated by latent-state trajectories; H2, reasoning is primarily mediated by explicit surface CoT; and H0, most apparent reasoning gains are better explained by generic serial compute than by any privileged representational object. Reorganizing recent empirical, mechanistic, and survey work under this framework, and adding compute-audited worked exemplars that factorize surface traces, latent interventions, and matched budget expansions, we find that current evidence most strongly supports H1 as a default working hypothesis rather than as a task-independent verdict. We therefore make two recommendations: the field should treat latent-state dynamics as the default object of study for LLM reasoning, and it should evaluate reasoning with designs that explicitly disentangle surface traces, latent states, and serial compute.

</details>


### [139] [Structured Abductive-Deductive-Inductive Reasoning for LLMs via Algebraic Invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15727)
*Sankalp Gilda,Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个符号推理框架，通过皮尔士三分推理范式和五个代数不变量（Gamma Quintet），解决了大语言模型在结构化逻辑推理中的系统性局限，特别是通过“最弱环节”界限防止错误在推理链中累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在结构化逻辑推理中存在系统性局限：混淆假设生成与验证过程，无法区分猜想与已验证知识，并且允许薄弱的推理步骤在推理链中不受控制地传播，导致逻辑不一致性累积。

Method: 提出了一个符号推理支架，将皮尔士的三分推理（溯因、演绎、归纳）操作化为LLM辅助推理的显式协议。框架通过五个代数不变量（Gamma Quintet）强制逻辑一致性，其中最强的“最弱环节”界限确保推理链中的结论可靠性不超过其支持度最低的前提。该方法在可能性逻辑中有独立的理论基础，并在思维链推理中得到了经验验证。

Result: 通过包含100个属性和16个模糊测试的基于属性的测试套件验证了所有不变量，测试覆盖了超过10^5个生成案例。提供了一个经过验证的不变量参考实现，可作为未来推理基准的基础。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将逻辑推理的严谨性引入LLM辅助推理过程，通过代数不变量确保逻辑一致性，为构建可靠的AI推理系统提供了经过验证的技术基础和基准参考。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit systematic limitations in structured logical reasoning: they conflate hypothesis generation with verification, cannot distinguish conjecture from validated knowledge, and allow weak reasoning steps to propagate unchecked through inference chains. We present a symbolic reasoning scaffold that operationalizes Peirce's tripartite inference -- abduction, deduction, and induction -- as an explicit protocol for LLM-assisted reasoning. The framework enforces logical consistency through five algebraic invariants (the Gamma Quintet), the strongest of which -- the Weakest Link bound -- ensures that no conclusion in a reasoning chain can exceed the reliability of its least-supported premise. This principle, independently grounded as weakest link resolution in possibilistic logic and empirically validated for chain-of-thought reasoning, prevents logical inconsistencies from accumulating across multi-step inference. We verify all invariants through a property-based testing suite of 100 properties and 16 fuzz tests over 10^5+ generated cases, providing a verified reference implementation of the invariants suitable as a foundation for future reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [140] [KWBench: Measuring Unprompted Problem Recognition in Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15760)
*Ankit Maloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了KWBench，首个评估大语言模型在无提示情况下识别专业问题能力的基准，包含223个来自实务的任务。最佳模型通过率仅27.9%，但多模型路由可将覆盖率提升至50.7%，揭示了当前模型在问题识别上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前沿基准已经饱和，大多数知识工作评估局限于信息提取或按照规范完成任务。然而，在实际应用中，模型首先需要从原始输入中识别问题的本质结构，这一步骤在现有评估中被忽视。因此，作者旨在填补这一空白，评估模型在未被明确告知问题类型时的识别能力。

Method: 构建包含223个任务的基准，涵盖收购、合同谈判、临床药学、组织政治、欺诈分析和激励设计等领域。每个任务编码了形式化的博弈论模式（如委托代理冲突、信号传递、机制设计失败等），并包含专家解读的结构化基本事实和预期失败模式。模型仅接收原始数据和任务提示，不提供问题类型信息。采用三级评分标准，包括强制性的合取检查，确保评分的严谨性。

Result: 评估了16个模型，最佳模型仅通过27.9%的任务。前两名模型的一致通过率仅31.7%。在前8个模型中，有44个任务仅被单一模型解决；通过在前8模型间进行任务路由，可覆盖50.7%的基准，几乎翻倍于最佳单一模型。通过任务的模型在质量评分上趋于一致（约83%），但无条件评分差异显著。值得注意的是，当被明确询问时，模型能够正确表述相关博弈论概念，但在无提示情况下却无法应用。

Conclusion: KWBench的发布旨在改变前沿模型的评估方式，重点评估模型是否能够仅从情境中识别正确问题，而不仅仅是执行已明确框架化的问题。研究揭示了当前模型在无提示问题识别方面的局限性，强调了在知识工作中问题识别能力的重要性。

Abstract: We introduce the first version of KWBench (Knowledge Work Bench), a benchmark for unprompted problem recognition in large language models: can an LLM identify a professional scenario before attempting to solve it. Existing frontier benchmarks have saturated, and most knowledge-work evaluations to date reduce to extraction or task completion against a specification. KWBench targets the step before that: recognizing the governing structure of the situation from raw inputs alone.
  The benchmark contains 223 tasks sourced from practitioners across acquisitions, contract negotiations, clinical pharmacy, organizational politics, fraud analysis, and incentive design. Each task encodes a formal game-theoretic pattern (principal-agent conflict, signaling, mechanism design failure, strategic omission, coalitional dynamics, strategic interdependence) and carries structured ground truth recording the expert reading of the situation and the anticipated failure modes. Models receive raw data and a task prompt with no indication of problem type. Scoring is a three-tier rubric gated by a mandatory conjunctive check. Mandatory criteria encode the predicted wrong paths.
  We evaluate 16 models. The best model passes on 27.9% of tasks. The top two models agree on only 31.7% of their passes. Among the top 8, 44 tasks are solved by exactly one model; routing across the top 8 covers 50.7% of the benchmark, nearly double the best single model. Conditional on passing, quality scores converge (approx 83% across models); unconditional scores do not. Same models articulate the relevant game-theoretic concept correctly when asked, then fail to apply it unprompted. We release KWBench to shift how frontier models are evaluated on knowledge work, scoring them on whether they recognize the right problem from the situation alone, not only on how well they execute once the problem has been framed for them.

</details>


### [141] [Stein Variational Black-Box Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15837)
*Thomas Landais,Olivier Goudet,Adrien Goëffon,Frédéric Saubion,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将Stein算子引入分布估计算法（EDA），通过粒子间的排斥机制增强种群多样性，有效解决了高维组合黑盒优化中的早熟收敛问题，在大规模实例上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维组合黑盒优化需要在开发与探索之间取得平衡。尽管分布估计算法（EDA）提供了强大的基于模型的框架，但它们往往集中于单一区域，在面对复杂或多峰目标景观时容易导致过早收敛。

Method: 在参数空间中引入Stein算子，在粒子之间建立排斥机制，鼓励种群分散并联合探索适应度景观的多个模态，从而避免算法过早陷入局部最优。

Result: 在多种基准问题上的实证评估表明，该方法在性能上与领先的最新算法具有竞争力，在某些情况下甚至表现更优，特别是在大规模实例上优势明显。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，Stein变分梯度下降为解决大规模、计算昂贵的离散黑盒优化问题提供了一个极具潜力的发展方向。

Abstract: Combinatorial black-box optimization in high-dimensional settings demands a careful trade-off between exploiting promising regions of the search space and preserving sufficient exploration to identify multiple optima. Although Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms (EDAs) provide a powerful model-based framework, they often concentrate on a single region of interest, which may result in premature convergence when facing complex or multimodal objective landscapes. In this work, we incorporate the Stein operator to introduce a repulsive mechanism among particles in the parameter space, thereby encouraging the population to disperse and jointly explore several modes of the fitness landscape. Empirical evaluations across diverse benchmark problems show that the proposed method achieves performance competitive with, and in several cases superior to, leading state-of-the-art approaches, particularly on large-scale instances. These findings highlight the potential of Stein variational gradient descent as a promising direction for addressing large, computationally expensive, discrete black-box optimization problems.

</details>


### [142] [Discover and Prove: An Open-source Agentic Framework for Hard Mode Automated Theorem Proving in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15839)
*Chengwu Liu,Yichun Yin,Ye Yuan,Jiaxuan Xie,Botao Li,Siqi Li,Jianhao Shen,Yan Xu,Lifeng Shang,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了现有ATP基准测试普遍采用“简单模式”（答案已嵌入形式化陈述），导致对模型能力的乐观估计。作者提出“困难模式”（需独立发现答案）并发布相应基准，开发了DAP框架在困难模式下取得SOTA性能，同时暴露了LLM高答案准确率与形式化证明低通过率之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数自动定理证明（ATP）基准测试将最终答案嵌入形式化陈述中（称为“简单模式”），这简化了人类竞赛者面临的实际任务难度，可能导致对模型能力的过高估计。为了更真实地评估系统在独立发现答案并证明方面的能力，作者提出更严格的“困难模式”设定。

Method: 1. 发布MiniF2F-Hard和FIMO-Hard两个专家重标注的困难模式基准测试变体；2. 提出Discover And Prove (DAP)智能体框架，利用大语言模型的自然语言推理和显式自我反思来发现答案，然后将困难模式陈述重写为简单模式供现有ATP证明器使用。

Result: DAP框架在CombiBench上解决的问题从7个（先前SOTA，Pass@16）提升至10个；在PutnamBench上首次在困难模式下形式化证明了36个定理。更重要的是，研究发现最先进的LLM在相同问题上答案准确率超过80%，而形式化证明器通过率不足10%，揭示了困难模式基准能够测量的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 困难模式基准测试为评估ATP系统提供了更真实、更具挑战性的测试环境，能够有效暴露LLM推理能力与形式化证明能力之间的显著鸿沟，为未来研究指明了重要方向。

Abstract: Most ATP benchmarks embed the final answer within the formal statement -- a convention we call "Easy Mode" -- a design that simplifies the task relative to what human competitors face and may lead to optimistic estimates of model capability. We call the stricter, more realistic setting "Hard Mode": the system must independently discover the answer before constructing a formal proof. To enable Hard Mode research, we make two contributions. First, we release MiniF2F-Hard and FIMO-Hard, expert-reannotated Hard Mode variants of two widely-used ATP benchmarks. Second, we introduce Discover And Prove (DAP), an agentic framework that uses LLM natural-language reasoning with explicit self-reflection to discover answers, then rewrites Hard Mode statements into Easy Mode ones for existing ATP provers. DAP sets the state of the art: on CombiBench it raises solved problems from 7 (previous SOTA, Pass@16) to 10; on PutnamBench it is the first system to formally prove 36 theorems in Hard Mode -- while simultaneously revealing that state-of-the-art LLMs exceed 80% answer accuracy on the same problems where formal provers manage under 10%, exposing a substantial gap that Hard Mode benchmarks are uniquely suited to measure.

</details>


### [143] [Towards Rigorous Explainability by Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15898)
*Olivier Létoffé,Xuanxiang Huang,Joao Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文概述了在可解释人工智能（XAI）中，使用严谨的符号方法替代非严谨的非符号方法（如SHAP）进行特征重要性排序的研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非符号解释方法（如SHAP）在解释复杂机器学习模型时缺乏严谨性，甚至可能误导决策者。在高风险应用场景中，这种严谨性的缺失问题尤为严重。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性论文，系统性梳理了当前致力于使用严谨的符号方法进行可解释人工智能（XAI）的研究工作，具体聚焦于相对特征重要性归因任务。

Result: 论文总结了将符号方法应用于特征重要性排序的现有研究进展，为替代缺乏严谨性的非符号方法提供了理论与实践依据。

Conclusion: 严谨的符号方法有望成为解决高风险机器学习应用中解释性问题的有效替代方案，为构建可信赖的XAI系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: For around a decade, non-symbolic methods have been the option of choice when explaining complex machine learning (ML) models. Unfortunately, such methods lack rigor and can mislead human decision-makers. In high-stakes uses of ML, the lack of rigor is especially problematic. One prime example of provable lack of rigor is the adoption of Shapley values in explainable artificial intelligence (XAI), with the tool SHAP being a ubiquitous example. This paper overviews the ongoing efforts towards using rigorous symbolic methods of XAI as an alternative to non-rigorous non-symbolic approaches, concretely for assigning relative feature importance.

</details>


### [144] [Integrating Graphs, Large Language Models, and Agents: Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15951)
*Hamed Jelodar,Samita Bai,Mohammad Meymani,Parisa Hamedi,Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Ali Ghorbani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于图与大语言模型（Graph-LLM）集成方法的综述，系统梳理了设计选择，按目的、图模态和集成策略对现有方法进行分类，并分析了各技术在多领域中的适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管生成式AI和大语言模型与图表示的集成在推理、检索和决策等方面发展迅速，但目前缺乏清晰的指导，难以判断在不同应用场景下应何时、为何以及采用何种类型的图-LLM集成方法最为合适。

Method: 作者提出了一种结构化的分类框架：1. 按目的分类（推理、检索、生成、推荐）；2. 按图模态分类（知识图谱、场景图、交互图、因果图、依赖图）；3. 按集成策略分类（提示工程、增强、训练、智能体应用）。同时，调研了网络安全、医疗健康、材料科学、金融、机器人学及多模态环境等多个领域的代表性工作。

Result: 通过对不同领域的代表性工作进行映射分析，揭示了各类图-LLM集成技术的优势、局限性及其最佳适用场景，明确了任务需求、数据特征和推理复杂度与集成方法选择之间的关系。

Conclusion: 本综述旨在为研究人员提供一份实用的指南，帮助其根据具体的任务需求、数据特征和推理复杂度，选择最合适的图与大语言模型集成方法。

Abstract: Generative AI, particularly Large Language Models, increasingly integrates graph-based representations to enhance reasoning, retrieval, and structured decision-making. Despite rapid advances, there remains limited clarity regarding when, why, where, and what types of graph-LLM integrations are most appropriate across applications. This survey provides a concise, structured overview of the design choices underlying the integration of graphs with LLMs. We categorize existing methods based on their purpose (reasoning, retrieval, generation, recommendation), graph modality (knowledge graphs, scene graphs, interaction graphs, causal graphs, dependency graphs), and integration strategies (prompting, augmentation, training, or agent-based use). By mapping representative works across domains such as cybersecurity, healthcare, materials science, finance, robotics, and multimodal environments, we highlight the strengths, limitations, and best-fit scenarios for each technique. This survey aims to offer researchers a practical guide for selecting the most suitable graph-LLM approach depending on task requirements, data characteristics, and reasoning complexity.

</details>


### [145] [ReactBench: A Benchmark for Topological Reasoning in MLLMs on Chemical Reaction Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15994)
*Qiang Xu,Shengyuan Bai,Yu Wang,He Cao,Leqing Chen,Yuanyuan Liu,Bin Feng,Zijing Liu,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReactBench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在复杂拓扑结构（如化学反应图）上的结构推理能力，发现现有模型在结构推理任务上存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在简单线性图推理上表现良好，但在涉及分支路径、汇聚流和循环依赖的复杂拓扑结构时，推理能力急剧下降。现有基准主要关注语义理解而非结构推理，无法揭示这一缺陷。

Method: 引入ReactBench基准，使用化学反应图作为测试对象。这些科学图表自然涵盖从线性链到循环图的多样化结构，同时需要精确的局部识别和连贯的全局推理。基准包含1,618个专家标注的问答对，跨越四个层次化的任务维度。对17个MLLM进行了广泛评估，并进行了受控消融实验。

Result: 评估揭示了基于锚点的任务与整体结构推理任务之间存在超过30%的显著性能差距。受控消融实验证实，性能瓶颈在于推理能力而非感知能力。

Conclusion: 研究发现暴露了多模态大语言模型在结构理解方面的根本性缺陷，为推进视觉推理研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at recognizing individual visual elements and reasoning over simple linear diagrams. However, when faced with complex topological structures involving branching paths, converging flows, and cyclic dependencies, their reasoning capabilities degrade sharply, even on tasks as basic as counting endpoints. Existing benchmarks fail to probe this gap, focusing on semantic comprehension rather than structural reasoning. We introduce ReactBench, a benchmark that reveals fundamental limitations in structural reasoning through chemical reaction diagrams. These real-world scientific diagrams offer an ideal testbed because they naturally span diverse structures from linear chains to cyclic graphs, while requiring both precise local recognition and coherent global reasoning. Our benchmark comprises 1,618 expert-annotated QA pairs across four hierarchical task dimensions. Extensive evaluation across 17 MLLMs reveals a significant performance gap exceeding 30% between anchor-based tasks and holistic structural reasoning tasks. Controlled ablations confirm this bottleneck lies in reasoning, not perception. These findings expose a fundamental deficit in structural understanding and establish directions for advancing visual reasoning.

</details>


### [146] [MEDLEY-BENCH: Scale Buys Evaluation but Not Control in AI Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16009)
*Farhad Abtahi,Abdolamir Karbalaie,Eduardo Illueca-Fernandez,Fernando Seoane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEDLEY-BENCH基准，用于评估AI的元认知能力。研究发现模型评估能力随规模增长，但控制能力不成比例；较小模型有时表现优于大模型；所有模型均存在“知行差距”，评估能力普遍最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元认知（监控和调节自身推理的能力）在AI基准测试中一直未得到充分评估。现有评估往往忽视模型在真实社交压力下的信念修正过程，缺乏对独立推理、自我修正和社会影响下修正的系统性区分与测量。

Method: 引入MEDLEY-BENCH基准，包含130个跨五个领域的模糊实例，涵盖独立推理、私密自我修正和社会影响下的修正三种条件。对来自12个家族的35个模型进行评估，提出两项互补指标：Medley元认知分数（MMS，基于反思性更新、社会稳健性和认知表达的分等级聚合）和Medley能力分数（MAS，源于四个元认知子能力）。进一步对11个模型进行渐进式对抗分析，并采用个体内相对分析（自比评分）进行能力画像。

Result: 1. 评估能力随模型规模增长而提升，但控制能力并不随之增强，呈现评估/控制分离；2. 模型表现出两种行为特征：基于论证质量修正型与跟踪共识统计型；3. 在所有35个模型中，评估能力均是最弱的相对能力，表明系统性的知行差距；4. 小型廉价模型常匹敌甚至超越大型模型，说明元认知能力并非简单随规模线性增长。

Conclusion: MEDLEY-BENCH为测量社交压力下的信念修正提供了有效工具。研究建议未来训练应奖励校准合理、成比例的信念更新，而非仅追求输出质量，从而促进模型元认知能力的发展。

Abstract: Metacognition, the ability to monitor and regulate one's own reasoning, remains under-evaluated in AI benchmarking. We introduce MEDLEY-BENCH, a benchmark of behavioural metacognition that separates independent reasoning, private self-revision, and socially influenced revision under genuine inter-model disagreement. The benchmark evaluates 35 models from 12 families on 130 ambiguous instances across five domains and reports two complementary scores: the Medley Metacognition Score (MMS), a tier-based aggregate of reflective updating, social robustness, and epistemic articulation, and the Medley Ability Score (MAS), derived from four metacognitive sub-abilities. Results show a robust evaluation/control dissociation: evaluation ability increases with model size within families, whereas control does not. In a follow-up progressive adversarial analysis of 11 models, we observed two behavioural profiles, i.e., models that revise primarily in response to argument quality and models that track consensus statistics. Under within-model relative profiling (ipsative scoring), evaluation was the weakest relative ability in all 35 models, indicating a systematic knowing/doing gap. Smaller and cheaper models often matched or outperformed larger counterparts, suggesting that metacognitive competence is not simply a function of scale. These findings position MEDLEY-BENCH as a tool for measuring belief revision under social pressure and suggest that future training should reward calibrated, proportional updating rather than output quality alone.

</details>


### [147] [MARCH: Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy for CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16175)
*Yi Lin,Yihao Ding,Yonghui Wu,Yifan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARCH框架，这是一个模拟放射科临床层级的多智能体系统，旨在通过模拟真实的临床协作流程来减少3D影像报告生成中的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化3D放射报告生成方法常出现临床幻觉，且缺乏临床实践中的迭代验证机制。此外，目前的视觉语言模型多为“黑盒”系统，缺乏临床工作流程中典型的协作监督机制。

Method: 提出MARCH（多智能体放射临床层级）框架，模拟放射科的专业层级。该框架包含：负责基于多尺度CT特征提取进行初稿撰写的住院医师智能体、利用检索增强进行修订的多位 fellow 智能体，以及一位负责协调迭代式、基于立场的共识讨论以解决诊断差异的主治医师智能体。

Result: 在RadGenome-ChestCT数据集上，MARCH在临床保真度和语言准确性方面均显著优于现有的最先进基线模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明，模拟类人的组织结构能够有效提高高风险医疗领域中人工智能系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Automated 3D radiology report generation often suffers from clinical hallucinations and a lack of the iterative verification found in human practice. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced the field, they typically operate as monolithic "black-box" systems without the collaborative oversight characteristic of clinical workflows. To address these challenges, we propose MARCH (Multi-Agent Radiology Clinical Hierarchy), a multi-agent framework that emulates the professional hierarchy of radiology departments and assigns specialized roles to distinct agents. MARCH utilizes a Resident Agent for initial drafting with multi-scale CT feature extraction, multiple Fellow Agents for retrieval-augmented revision, and an Attending Agent that orchestrates an iterative, stance-based consensus discourse to resolve diagnostic discrepancies. On the RadGenome-ChestCT dataset, MARCH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both clinical fidelity and linguistic accuracy. Our work demonstrates that modeling human-like organizational structures enhances the reliability of AI in high-stakes medical domains.

</details>


### [148] [Characterising LLM-Generated Competency Questions: a Cross-Domain Empirical Study using Open and Closed Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16258)
*Reham Alharbi,Valentina Tamma,Terry R. Payne,Jacopo de Berardinis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套量化指标，用于系统性地评估大语言模型（LLM）生成的本体工程能力问题（CQs），实验分析了多种开源与闭源模型的生成特性，发现不同模型在不同用例下表现出独特的生成配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能力问题（CQs）是本体工程需求获取的核心，传统人工建模方式门槛高。生成式AI虽能自动化规模化创建CQs以降低门槛，但面对参数规模、专业化程度及可访问性各异的大规模LLM，亟需系统性地刻画和比较其生成CQs的内在可观测属性（如可读性、结构复杂度）。

Method: 提出一套用于跨多维度系统性比较CQs的量化指标；基于明确界定的用例与场景，使用多个LLM（开源模型KimiK2-1T、LLama3.1-8B、LLama3.2-3B及闭源模型Gemini 2.5 Pro、GPT 4.1）生成CQs，并对其可读性、与输入文本的相关性及结构复杂度等显著属性进行分析。

Result: 实验识别了不同LLM生成的CQs的显著属性；分析表明，LLM的性能反映了由用例塑造的独特生成配置，即不同模型在不同用例下呈现出差异化的生成特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和刻画LLM生成CQs的特性提供了系统的量化评估框架，有助于在Ontology工程实践中根据具体用例需求选择合适的LLM，推动了CQ生成过程的民主化。

Abstract: Competency Questions (CQs) are a cornerstone of requirement elicitation in ontology engineering. CQs represent requirements as a set of natural language questions that an ontology should satisfy; they are traditionally modelled by ontology engineers together with domain experts as part of a human-centred, manual elicitation process. The use of Generative AI automates CQ creation at scale, therefore democratising the process of generation, widening stakeholder engagement, and ultimately broadening access to ontology engineering. However, given the large and heterogeneous landscape of LLMs, varying in dimensions such as parameter scale, task and domain specialisation, and accessibility, it is crucial to characterise and understand the intrinsic, observable properties of the CQs they produce (e.g., readability, structural complexity) through a systematic, cross-domain analysis. This paper introduces a set of quantitative measures for the systematic comparison of CQs across multiple dimensions. Using CQs generated from well defined use cases and scenarios, we identify their salient properties, including readability, relevance with respect to the input text and structural complexity of the generated questions. We conduct our experiments over a set of use cases and requirements using a range of LLMs, including both open (KimiK2-1T, LLama3.1-8B, LLama3.2-3B) and closed models (Gemini 2.5 Pro, GPT 4.1). Our analysis demonstrates that LLM performance reflects distinct generation profiles shaped by the use case.

</details>


### [149] [Learning to Reason with Insight for Informal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16278)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Bowen Deng,Wei Wang,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Siyang Gao,Chao Wang,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepInsightTheorem数据集和渐进式多阶段监督微调策略，通过显式提取核心技术并引导模型进行洞察性推理，有效解决了非形式化定理证明中缺乏洞察力的瓶颈问题，显著提升了模型在数学推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动定理证明方法主要依赖形式化证明系统，但非形式化定理证明能更好地发挥大语言模型在自然语言处理方面的优势。然而，非形式化定理证明面临一个关键瓶颈：缺乏洞察力，即难以识别解决复杂问题所需的核心技术。

Method: 提出了DeepInsightTheorem分层级数据集，显式提取核心技术、证明草图和最终证明；设计了渐进式多阶段监督微调（SFT）策略，模拟人类学习过程，引导模型从基础证明写作逐步发展到洞察性思考。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学基准测试上的实验表明，这种洞察感知的生成策略显著优于基线方法，证明了教导模型识别和应用核心技术可以大幅提升其数学推理能力。

Conclusion: 培养模型识别和应用核心技术的能力对于提升非形式化定理证明性能至关重要，通过结构化数据集和渐进式学习方法可以有效增强模型的洞察性推理能力。
